Report',{preflight:diag.warnings,parser:diag.parserWarnings,integrity:diag.integrity});if(isCompare){state.compareModel=m;state.compareProjId=m.table('PROJECT')[0]?.proj_id||null;toast(`Comparison schedule loaded: ${file.name}`);setView('compare');return}state.model=m;state.sourceFormat=m.sourceFormat||'xer';state.history=new EditHistory(m);state.dirty=false;state.baselines=[];state.primaryBaselineId=null;state.selectedBaselineId=null;state.lastCalc=null;state.fileName=file.name;state.projId=m.table('PROJECT')[0]?.proj_id||m.table('TASK')[0]?.proj_id||null;state.selectedTaskId=m.table('TASK')[0]?.task_id||null;state.selectedWbsId=wbsRows(m,state.projId)[0]?.wbs_id||null;state.selectedFloatTarget=state.selectedTaskId;state.search='';state.revisionSnapshots=[snapshotModel(m,`Loaded ${file.name}`)];state.revisionRepository=[createRevision(m,{name:`Loaded ${file.name}`,projectId:state.projId,sourceFile:file.name})];state.selectedRevisionA=state.revisionRepository[0]?.id||'';state.selectedRevisionB='';state.forensicComparison=null;state.dateMoveResult=null;state.audit=new AuditLog();state.audit.add('Open schedule',{file:file.name,format:state.sourceFormat});const integrity=validateModelIntegrity(m,{projectId:state.projId});if(integrity.issues.length)console.warn('Schedule integrity findings',integrity);$('#fileStatus').textContent=`${file.name} • ${formatLabel(state.sourceFormat)} • ${m.rowCount().toLocaleString()} canonical rows${integrity.counts.errors?` • ${integrity.counts.errors} integrity error(s)`:''}${diag.warnings.length||diag.parserWarnings.length?` • ${diag.warnings.length+diag.parserWarnings.length} import warning(s)`:''}`;populateProjects();$('#exportBtn').disabled=false;updateEditButtons();crumb();render();toast(`Loaded ${file.name}`);logger.end('loadFile','app','loadFile','Schedule loaded')}catch(e){logger.error('app','loadFile',String(e?.message||e),{file:file?.name||'',compare:isCompare});console.error(e);alert(`Could not load schedule:\n${e.message}`)}}
function populateProjects(){const s=$('#projectSelect'), rows=projectRows(state.model);s.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(p=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(p.proj_id)}">${escapeHtml(p.proj_short_name||p.proj_name||p.proj_id)}</option>`).join(''):`<option value="${escapeHtml(state.projId||'')}">Project ${escapeHtml(state.projId||'')}</option>`;s.disabled=false;s.value=state.projId||''}

function render(){crumb();if(!state.model && !['settings','tutorial','bimModel'].includes(state.view)){return}
 const fn={dashboard:renderDashboard,eps:renderEPS,activities:renderActivities,wbs:renderWBS,relationships:renderRelationships,resources:renderResources,resourceSheet:renderResourceSheet,resourceProfiles:renderResourceProfiles,bim:renderBIM,bimModel:renderBimModel,tutorial:renderTutorial,calendars:renderCalendars,codes:renderCodes,analysis:renderAnalysis,baselines:renderBaselines,compare:renderCompare,raw:renderRaw,editor:renderEditor,settings:renderSettings,layouts:renderLayouts,diagnostics:renderDiagnostics,progressEvm:renderProgressEvm,history:renderHistory,workspace:renderWorkspace,intelligence:renderIntelligence,health:renderHealthRisk,trends:renderTrends,reporting:renderReporting,searchAll:renderGlobalSearch}[state.view];fn?.();}

function renderEPS(){
 const projects=projectRows(state.model);
 const rows=projects.map(p=>{
   const pid=p.proj_id,tasks=taskRows(state.model,pid),wbs=wbsRows(state.model,pid);
   const starts=tasks.map(t=>p6Date(t.target_start_date||t.early_start_date||t.act_start_date)).filter(Boolean),finishes=tasks.map(t=>p6Date(t.target_end_date||t.early_end_date||t.act_end_date)).filter(Boolean);
   const health=tasks.length?dcmaStyleHealth(state.model,pid,state.settings).score:100;
   const avgPct=tasks.length?tasks.reduce((n,t)=>n+num(t.phys_complete_pct||t.complete_pct),0)/tasks.length:0;
   return {...p,project_name:p.proj_short_name||p.proj_name||pid,activities:tasks.length,wbs_count:wbs.length,project_start:starts.length?new Date(Math.min(...starts)):null,forecast_finish:finishes.length?new Date(Math.max(...finishes)):null,health,avgPct};
 });
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel p6-projects-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Enterprise Project Structure</h3><span>Single-user local project database</span></div><span>${projects.length} project${projects.length===1?'':'s'}</span></div><div class="p6-project-split"><div class="p6-project-table">${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'project_name',label:'EPS / Project Name'},{key:'health',label:'Score',num:true},{key:'avgPct',label:'% Complete',num:true,render:r=>`${r.avgPct.toFixed(1)}%`},{key:'last_recalc_date',label:'Data Date',render:r=>fmtDate(r.last_recalc_date||r.data_date)},{key:'activities',label:'Activities',num:true},{key:'forecast_finish',label:'Forecast Finish',render:r=>fmtDate(r.forecast_finish)}],{selectId:'proj_id'})}</div><div class="p6-project-gantt">${projectGanttHTML(rows)}</div></div></div>`;
 $$('#view tbody tr[data-id]').forEach(tr=>tr.onclick=()=>{state.projId=tr.dataset.id;$('#projectSelect').value=state.projId;state.selectedTaskId=taskRows(state.model,state.projId)[0]?.task_id||null;setView('activities')});
}
function projectGanttHTML(rows){if(!rows.length)return '<div class="empty">No projects.</div>';const valid=rows.filter(r=>r.project_start&&r.forecast_finish);if(!valid.length)return '<div class="empty">No valid project dates.</div>';const min=new Date(Math.min(...valid.map(r=>r.project_start))),max=new Date(Math.max(...valid.map(r=>r.forecast_finish))),span=Math.max(86400000,max-min),start=new Date(min-span*.03),finish=new Date(max.getTime()+span*.03),total=finish-start,pos=d=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(d-start)/total*100));const months=[];let c=new Date(start.getFullYear(),start.getMonth(),1);while(c<=finish){const n=new Date(c.getFullYear(),c.getMonth()+1,1);months.push({label:c.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{month:'short',year:'2-digit'}),left:pos(c),width:Math.max(1,pos(n)-pos(c))});c=n}return `<div class="p6-project-gantt-head">${months.map(m=>`<span style="left:${m.left}%;width:${m.width}%">${m.label}</span>`).join('')}</div><div>${rows.map(r=>{if(!r.project_start||!r.forecast_finish)return '<div class="p6-project-gantt-row"></div>';const left=pos(r.project_start),width=Math.max(.5,pos(r.forecast_finish)-left);return `<div class="p6-project-gantt-row"><i style="left:${left}%;width:${width}%" title="${escapeHtml(r.project_name)}"></i><span style="left:${Math.min(96,left+width+.5)}%">${escapeHtml(r.project_name)}</span></div>`}).join('')}</div>`}

function renderDashboard(){
 const tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId),rels=predRows(state.model,state.projId),analysis=analyzeSchedule(state.model,state.projId,state.settings),project=currentProject(),baseline=primaryBaseline();
 const starts=tasks.map(t=>new Date(t.target_start_date||t.early_start_date)).filter(d=>!isNaN(d)),fins=tasks.map(t=>new Date(t.target_end_date||t.early_end_date)).filter(d=>!isNaN(d));
 const start=starts.length?new Date(Math.min(...starts)):null,finish=fins.length?new Date(Math.max(...fins)):null;
 const exec=executiveDashboard(state.model,state.projId,{baseline,settings:state.settings});
 const healthItems=Object.entries(analysis.byRule).sort((a,b)=>b[1].length-a[1].length).slice(0,6);
 const baselineText=exec.finishVarianceDays===null?'No baseline selected':`${exec.finishVarianceDays>=0?'+':''}${exec.finishVarianceDays.toFixed(1)} calendar days`;
 const statusClass=exec.scheduleHealth>=85?'ok':exec.scheduleHealth>=70?'medium':'high';
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="dashboard-shell">
   <section class="dashboard-project-header">
     <div><div class="eyebrow">Current Project</div><h2>${escapeHtml(project?.proj_short_name||project?.proj_name||'Schedule')}</h2><div class="dashboard-meta"><span>Data Date <b>${escapeHtml(fmtDate(analysis.dataDate))}</b></span><span>Start <b>${escapeHtml(fmtDate(start))}</b></span><span>Finish <b>${escapeHtml(fmtDate(finish))}</b></span></div></div>
     <div class="dashboard-status"><span class="status-pill ${statusClass}">Health ${exec.scheduleHealth}/100</span><span class="status-pill">${tasks.length.toLocaleString()} activities</span></div>
   </section>
   <section class="metric-grid">
     <div class="metric-card emphasis"><span>Forecast Completion</span><strong>${escapeHtml(fmtDate(exec.currentFinish||finish))}</strong><small>Current schedule</small></div>
     <div class="metric-card ${exec.finishVarianceDays>0?'risk':''}"><span>Baseline Variance</span><strong>${escapeHtml(baselineText)}</strong><small>${baseline?'Selected primary baseline':'Load a baseline to measure variance'}</small></div>
     <div class="metric-card ${exec.critical>0?'risk':''}"><span>Critical / ≤0 Float</span><strong>${exec.critical.toLocaleString()}</strong><small>${exec.nearCritical.toLocaleString()} near-critical</small></div>
     <div class="metric-card"><span>Logic Density</span><strong>${(rels.length/Math.max(1,tasks.length)).toFixed(2)}</strong><small>${rels.length.toLocaleString()} relationships</small></div>
   </section>
   <section class="dashboard-grid">
     <div class="panel dashboard-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Project Snapshot</h3><span>Key schedule context</span></div><button data-jump="activities">Open Activities</button></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="snapshot-grid"><div><span>Project ID</span><b>${escapeHtml(project?.proj_id||'—')}</b></div><div><span>WBS Nodes</span><b>${wbsRows(state.model,state.projId).length.toLocaleString()}</b></div><div><span>Calendars</span><b>${state.model.table('CALENDAR').length.toLocaleString()}</b></div><div><span>Resources</span><b>${state.model.table('RSRC').length.toLocaleString()}</b></div><div><span>Average Progress</span><b>${Number(exec.averageProgress||0).toFixed(1)}%</b></div><div><span>Canonical Records</span><b>${state.model.rowCount().toLocaleString()}</b></div></div></div></div>
     <div class="panel dashboard-panel attention-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Planner Attention</h3><span>Highest-volume schedule findings</span></div><button data-jump="analysis">Review Analysis</button></div><div class="attention-list">${healthItems.map(([rule,items])=>`<button data-jump="analysis"><span>${escapeHtml(rule)}</span><b>${items.length.toLocaleString()}</b></button>`).join('')||'<div class="empty compact">No schedule-health findings.</div>'}</div></div>
   </section>
   <section class="panel dashboard-gantt-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Schedule Timeline</h3><span>Full-project Gantt overview</span></div><button data-jump="activities">Detailed Gantt</button></div><div id="dashGantt" class="gantt dashboard-gantt"></div></section>
 </div>`;
 renderGantt($('#dashGantt'),tasks,{hoursPerDay:state.settings.hoursPerDay,baselineMap:baseline?baselineOverlayMap(baseline):null,showBaseline:state.showBaselineBars,dataDate:getDataDate(state.model,state.projId),onSelect:id=>{state.selectedTaskId=id;setView('activities')}});
 $$('[data-jump]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setView(b.dataset.jump));
}

function renderActivities(){
 let tasks=filterTasks();const f=state.sort.field,d=state.sort.dir;tasks=[...tasks].sort((a,b)=>String(a[f]??'').localeCompare(String(b[f]??''),undefined,{numeric:true})*d);
 const selected=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',state.selectedTaskId)||tasks[0];if(selected)state.selectedTaskId=selected.task_id;
 const pbl=primaryBaseline(),pblById=pbl?new Map(baselineRows(state.model,state.projId,pbl,state.settings.hoursPerDay).filter(x=>x.task_id).map(x=>[String(x.task_id),x])):new Map();
 const labels={task_code:'Activity ID',task_name:'Activity Name',status_code:'Status',task_type:'Type',target_drtn_hr_cnt:'Original Duration',remain_drtn_hr_cnt:'Remaining Duration',early_start_date:'Start',early_end_date:'Finish',target_start_date:'Planned Start',target_end_date:'Planned Finish',total_float_hr_cnt:'Total Float',free_float_hr_cnt:'Free Float',wbs_id:'WBS',clndr_id:'Calendar',phys_complete_pct:'Activity % Complete'};
 const layout=state.layouts.find(x=>x.id===state.activeLayoutId)||defaultLayout();
 const preferred=['task_code','task_name','target_drtn_hr_cnt','remain_drtn_hr_cnt','early_start_date','early_end_date','phys_complete_pct','total_float_hr_cnt'];
 const colKeys=(layout.id==='default'?preferred:(layout.columns||preferred)).filter(k=>k in (tasks[0]||{})||labels[k]);
 const cols=colKeys.map(key=>({key,label:labels[key]||key,num:/drtn|float|pct|qty|cost/i.test(key),render:r=>key==='wbs_id'?escapeHtml(wbsDisplayLabel(state.model,r.wbs_id)):key==='clndr_id'?escapeHtml(calendarDisplayLabel(state.model,r.clndr_id)):/date/.test(key)?fmtDate(r[key]):escapeHtml(r[key]??'—')}));
 if(pbl)cols.push({key:'blFinish',label:'BL Finish',render:r=>fmtDate(pblById.get(String(r.task_id))?.baseline_finish)},{key:'finishVar',label:'Finish Var',render:r=>{const v=pblById.get(String(r.task_id))?.finish_variance_days;return Number.isFinite(v)?`<span class="${v>0?'baseline-late':v<0?'baseline-ahead':''}">${v>0?'+':''}${v.toFixed(1)}d</span>`:'—'}});
 const statusOptions=[...new Set(taskRows(state.model,state.projId).map(t=>t.status_code).filter(Boolean))];
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="p6-activity-workspace">
   <div class="p6-layout-strip"><div class="p6-layout-left"><label>Layout: <select id="activityLayout">${state.layouts.map(x=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(x.id)}" ${x.id===state.activeLayoutId?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Filter: <select id="activityStatus"><option value="">All Activities</option>${statusOptions.map(x=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(x)}">${escapeHtml(x)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button id="groupSortBtn">Group & Sort…</button></div><div class="p6-layout-right"><span>${tasks.length.toLocaleString()} activities</span><button id="showBaselineToggle">${state.showBaselineBars?'Hide':'Show'} Baseline</button><button id="zoomOutBtn">−</button><button id="zoomInBtn">+</button></div></div>
   <div class="p6-activity-main" id="activityMain"><div id="activityTable" class="p6-grid-pane">${groupedTableHTML(tasks,cols,layout.groupBy,'task_id')}</div><div id="activitySplitter" class="p6-splitter" title="Drag to resize table / Gantt"></div><div id="activityGantt" class="gantt p6-gantt-pane"></div></div>
   <div class="p6-detail-pane" id="detailPanel"></div>
 </div>`;
 bindActivityRows();
 renderGantt($('#activityGantt'),tasks,{selectedTaskId:state.selectedTaskId,baselineMap:pbl?baselineOverlayMap(pbl):null,showBaseline:state.showBaselineBars,dataDate:getDataDate(state.model,state.projId),groupBy:layout.groupBy,groupLabel:t=>groupDisplayLabel(state.model,layout.groupBy,t),relationships:predRows(state.model,state.projId),zoom:state.ganttZoom,onSelect:selectTask});
 renderActivityDetail(selected);
 bindActivitySplitAndScroll();
 $('#activityStatus').onchange=e=>{const v=e.target.value;$('#activityTable tbody')?.querySelectorAll('tr[data-id]').forEach(tr=>{const t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',tr.dataset.id);tr.hidden=!!v&&t.status_code!==v});};
 $('#activityLayout').onchange=e=>{state.activeLayoutId=e.target.value;renderActivities()};
 $('#groupSortBtn').onclick=()=>setView('layouts');
 $('#showBaselineToggle').onclick=()=>{state.showBaselineBars=!state.showBaselineBars;renderActivities()};
 $('#zoomInBtn').onclick=()=>{state.ganttZoom=Math.min(4,(state.ganttZoom||1)*1.5);if($('#ganttZoom'))$('#ganttZoom').value=String([1,1.5,2,4].reduce((a,b)=>Math.abs(b-state.ganttZoom)<Math.abs(a-state.ganttZoom)?b:a));renderActivities()};
 $('#zoomOutBtn').onclick=()=>{state.ganttZoom=Math.max(1,(state.ganttZoom||1)/1.5);renderActivities()};
}
function bindActivitySplitAndScroll(){
 const main=$('#activityMain'),splitter=$('#activitySplitter'),tableScroll=$('#activityTable .table-wrap'),gantt=$('#activityGantt');if(!main||!splitter||!tableScroll||!gantt)return;
 let lock=false;const sync=(from,to)=>{if(lock)return;lock=true;to.scrollTop=from.scrollTop;requestAnimationFrame(()=>lock=false)};tableScroll.addEventListener('scroll',()=>sync(tableScroll,gantt),{passive:true});gantt.addEventListener('scroll',()=>sync(gantt,tableScroll),{passive:true});
 splitter.onpointerdown=e=>{e.preventDefault();splitter.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);const rect=main.getBoundingClientRect(),start=e.clientX,startWidth=tableScroll.closest('#activityTable').getBoundingClientRect().width;const move=ev=>{const width=Math.max(360,Math.min(rect.width-420,startWidth+(ev.clientX-start)));main.style.setProperty('--activity-table-width',`${width}px`)};const up=ev=>{splitter.releasePointerCapture?.(ev.pointerId);splitter.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);splitter.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);splitter.removeEventListener('pointercancel',up)};splitter.addEventListener('pointermove',move);splitter.addEventListener('pointerup',up);splitter.addEventListener('pointercancel',up)};
}
function bindActivityRows(){$$('#activityTable tbody tr[data-id]').forEach(tr=>tr.onclick=()=>selectTask(tr.dataset.id))}
function selectTask(id){state.selectedTaskId=id;const t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',id);renderActivityDetail(t);$$('#activityTable tbody tr').forEach(r=>r.classList.toggle('selected',r.dataset.id===id));const layout=state.layouts.find(x=>x.id===state.activeLayoutId)||defaultLayout();renderGantt($('#activityGantt'),filterTasks(),{selectedTaskId:id,baselineMap:primaryBaseline()?baselineOverlayMap(primaryBaseline()):null,showBaseline:state.showBaselineBars,dataDate:getDataDate(state.model,state.projId),groupBy:layout.groupBy,groupLabel:x=>groupDisplayLabel(state.model,layout.groupBy,x),relationships:predRows(state.model,state.projId),zoom:state.ganttZoom,onSelect:selectTask})}
function renderActivityDetail(task){
 const el=$('#detailPanel');if(!task){el.innerHTML='<div class="empty">Select an activity.</div>';return}const a=activityView(state.model,task);
 el.innerHTML=`<div class="panel-head"><h3>${escapeHtml(a.id)} — ${escapeHtml(a.name)}</h3><span class="badge ${a.totalFloat<=0?'high':''}">${a.totalFloat<=0?'Critical / ≤0 float':'Non-critical'}</span></div><div class="tabs"><button class="active" data-tab="general">General</button><button data-tab="status">Status</button><button data-tab="dates">Dates</button><button data-tab="logic">Relationships</button><button data-tab="resources">Resources</button><button data-tab="expenses">Expenses</button><button data-tab="codes">Codes</button><button data-tab="constraints">Constraints</button><button data-tab="notes">Notebooks</button><button data-tab="steps">Steps</button><button data-tab="risks">Risks</button><button data-tab="trace">Trace Logic</button><button data-tab="raw">Raw Data</button></div><div class="detail" id="detailBody"></div>`;
 const draw=tab=>{const b=$('#detailBody');
  if(tab==='general')b.innerHTML=kv([['Activity Type',a.type],['Duration Type',a.raw.duration_type||a.raw.task_type||'—'],['% Complete Type',a.pctType],['Activity Calendar',a.calendarName],['WBS',`${a.wbsCode||''} ${a.wbsName||''}`],['Project',a.projectName],['Original Duration',`${a.originalDuration} h`],['Remaining Duration',`${a.remainingDuration} h`]]);
  if(tab==='status')b.innerHTML=kv([['Status',a.status],['Activity % Complete',`${a.pct}%`],['Original Duration',`${a.originalDuration} h`],['Remaining Duration',`${a.remainingDuration} h`],['Actual Duration',`${a.actualDuration} h`],['Total Float',`${a.totalFloat} h`],['Free Float',`${a.freeFloat} h`],['Calendar',a.calendarName]]);if(tab==='dates')b.innerHTML=kv([['Planned Start',fmtDate(a.raw.target_start_date,true)],['Planned Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.target_end_date,true)],['Early Start',fmtDate(a.raw.early_start_date,true)],['Early Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.early_end_date,true)],['Late Start',fmtDate(a.raw.late_start_date,true)],['Late Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.late_end_date,true)],['Actual Start',fmtDate(a.raw.act_start_date,true)],['Actual Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.act_end_date,true)]]);
  if(tab==='logic')b.innerHTML=relationshipMini(a);
  if(tab==='resources')b.innerHTML=resourceMini(a);if(tab==='expenses')b.innerHTML=genericTaskTable('PROJCOST',task.task_id,'No activity expenses are stored in this schedule.');
  if(tab==='codes')b.innerHTML=codeMini(a);
  if(tab==='constraints')b.innerHTML=kv([['Primary Constraint',a.primaryConstraint],['Constraint Date',fmtDate(a.primaryConstraintDate,true)],['Secondary Constraint',a.secondaryConstraint],['Constraint 2 Date',fmtDate(a.secondaryConstraintDate,true)],['Expected Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.expect_end_date,true)],['Suspend Date',fmtDate(a.raw.suspend_date,true)],['Resume Date',fmtDate(a.raw.resume_date,true)],['External Finish',fmtDate(a.raw.external_early_end_date||a.raw.external_late_end_date,true)]]);
  if(tab==='notes')b.innerHTML=noteMini(task.task_id);if(tab==='steps')b.innerHTML=genericTaskTable('TASKSTEP',task.task_id,'No activity steps are stored in this schedule.');if(tab==='risks')b.innerHTML=riskMini(task.task_id);
  if(tab==='trace'){const diag=explainActivity(state.model,state.projId,task.task_id,state.lastCalc);b.innerHTML=`<div class="trace-summary"><b>Driving predecessor assessment</b><p>${escapeHtml(driverText(diag))}</p></div>${tableHTML(diag?.incoming||[],[{key:'predId',label:'Predecessor'},{key:'predName',label:'Name'},{key:'type',label:'Rel'},{key:'lagHours',label:'Lag h',num:true},{key:'predStart',label:'Pred Start',render:r=>fmtDate(r.predStart)},{key:'predFinish',label:'Pred Finish',render:r=>fmtDate(r.predFinish)}])}`;}
  if(tab==='raw')b.innerHTML=`<pre style="white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(a.raw,null,2))}</pre>`;
 };
 draw('general');$$('#detailPanel [data-tab]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{$$('#detailPanel [data-tab]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===btn));draw(btn.dataset.tab)})
}
function noteMini(taskId){const memos=(state.model.table('TASKMEMO')||[]).filter(r=>String(r.task_id)===String(taskId));if(!memos.length)return '<div class="empty compact">No activity notebook topics.</div>';const types=new Map((state.model.table('MEMOTYPE')||[]).map(x=>[String(x.memo_type_id),x]));return tableHTML(memos.map(r=>({...r,topic:types.get(String(r.memo_type_id))?.memo_type_name||types.get(String(r.memo_type_id))?.memo_type||r.memo_type_id,text:r.task_memo||r.memo_text||r.memo||''})),[{key:'topic',label:'Notebook Topic'},{key:'text',label:'Text'}])}
function genericTaskTable(tableName,taskId,emptyText){const rows=(state.model.table(tableName)||[]).filter(r=>String(r.task_id??r.fk_id??'')===String(taskId));if(!rows.length)return `<div class="empty compact">${escapeHtml(emptyText)}</div>`;const keys=Object.keys(rows[0]).filter(k=>!k.startsWith('__')).slice(0,12);return tableHTML(rows,keys.map(k=>({key:k,label:k.replaceAll('_',' ').replace(/\b\w/g,c=>c.toUpperCase())}))) }
function riskMini(taskId){const rows=(state.model.table('RISK')||[]).filter(r=>[r.task_id,r.risk_start_task_id,r.risk_end_task_id,r.pre_task_id,r.post_task_id].some(v=>String(v??'')===String(taskId)));if(!rows.length)return '<div class="empty compact">No activity-linked risks are stored in this schedule.</div>';const keys=Object.keys(rows[0]).filter(k=>!k.startsWith('__')).slice(0,12);return tableHTML(rows,keys.map(k=>({key:k,label:k.replaceAll('_',' ').replace(/\b\w/g,c=>c.toUpperCase())}))) }
function kv(rows){return `<div class="kv">${rows.map(([k,v])=>`<b>${escapeHtml(k)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(v||'—')}</span>`).join('')}</div>`}
function relationshipMini(a){const row=(r,pred)=>{const id=pred?r.pred_task_id:r.task_id,t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',id);return `<tr data-id="${escapeHtml(id)}"><td>${escapeHtml(t?.task_code||id)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t?.task_name||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.pred_type||'')}</td><td class="num">${escapeHtml(r.lag_hr_cnt||0)}</td></tr>`};return `<h4>Predecessors</h4><table><thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Lag h</th></tr></thead><tbody>${a.preds.map(r=>row(r,true)).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="4">None</td></tr>'}</tbody></table><h4>Successors</h4><table><thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Lag h</th></tr></thead><tbody>${a.succs.map(r=>row(r,false)).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="4">None</td></tr>'}</tbody></table>`}
function resourceMini(a){return tableHTML(a.resources.map(r=>{const res=state.model.find('RSRC','rsrc_id',r.rsrc_id);return {...r,rsrc_name:res?.rsrc_name||r.rsrc_id}}),[{key:'rsrc_name',label:'Resource'},{key:'target_qty',label:'Budget Units',num:true},{key:'act_reg_qty',label:'Actual Units',num:true},{key:'remain_qty',label:'Remaining Units',num:true},{key:'target_cost',label:'Budget Cost',num:true},{key:'act_reg_cost',label:'Actual Cost',num:true}])}
function codeMini(a){return `<h4>Activity Codes</h4>${tableHTML(a.codes,[{key:'type',label:'Code Type'},{key:'code',label:'Code'},{key:'description',label:'Description'}])}<h4>UDFs</h4>${tableHTML(a.udf,[{key:'title',label:'UDF'},{key:'value',label:'Value'}])}`}

function renderWBS(){
 const rows=wbsRows(state.model,state.projId),tree=buildWBSTree(state.model,state.projId),tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId);
 if(!rows.some(w=>w.wbs_id===state.selectedWbsId))state.selectedWbsId=rows[0]?.wbs_id||null;
 const selected=rows.find(w=>w.wbs_id===state.selectedWbsId)||null;
 const directCount=id=>tasks.filter(t=>String(t.wbs_id)===String(id)).length;
 const subtreeCount=id=>{const ids=new Set([String(id),...wbsDescendantIds(state.model,id)]);return tasks.filter(t=>ids.has(String(t.wbs_id))).length};
 const rec=(nodes,depth=0)=>nodes.map(n=>{const expanded=state.wbsExpanded[n.wbs_id]!==false;return `<div class="wbs-tree-row ${String(n.wbs_id)===String(state.selectedWbsId)?'selected':''}" draggable="true" data-wbs-id="${escapeHtml(n.wbs_id)}" style="--depth:${depth}"><span class="wbs-toggle" data-toggle-wbs="${escapeHtml(n.wbs_id)}">${n.children.length?(expanded?'▾':'▸'):'·'}</span><span class="wbs-code">${escapeHtml(n.wbs_short_name||n.wbs_id)}</span><span class="wbs-name">${escapeHtml(n.wbs_name||'')}</span><span class="wbs-count">${directCount(n.wbs_id)} direct / ${subtreeCount(n.wbs_id)} total</span></div>${expanded?rec(n.children,depth+1):''}`}).join('');
 const parentOptions=['<option value="">— Project root —</option>',...rows.filter(w=>!selected||w.wbs_id!==selected.wbs_id&&!wbsDescendantIds(state.model,selected.wbs_id).includes(w.wbs_id)).map(w=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(w.wbs_id)}" ${selected&&String(selected.parent_wbs_id||'')===String(w.wbs_id)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(w.wbs_short_name||w.wbs_id)} — ${escapeHtml(w.wbs_name||'')}</option>`)].join('');
 const activityRows=selected?tasks.filter(t=>String(t.wbs_id)===String(selected.wbs_id)):[];
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="wbs-editor-layout">
 <div class="panel wbs-tree-panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${rows.length} WBS nodes</h3><div class="button-row"><button id="wbsAddRoot">+ Root</button><button id="wbsAddChild" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>+ Child</button><button id="wbsAddSibling" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>+ Sibling</button><button id="wbsCopy" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>Copy Branch</button><button id="wbsPaste" ${state.wbsClipboard&&selected?'':'disabled'}>Paste as Child</button><button id="wbsDuplicate" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>Duplicate</button><button id="wbsExpandAll">Expand All</button><button id="wbsCollapseAll">Collapse All</button></div></div><div class="wbs-tree">${rec(tree)||'<div class="empty">No WBS data. Add a root WBS to begin.</div>'}</div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${selected?`${escapeHtml(selected.wbs_short_name||selected.wbs_id)} — ${escapeHtml(selected.wbs_name||'')}`:'WBS Details'}</h3><div class="button-row"><button id="wbsSave" class="primary" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>Save</button><button id="wbsDelete" class="danger" ${selected?'':'disabled'}>Delete</button></div></div>
 ${selected?`<div class="panel-body"><div class="edit-form"><label>WBS Code</label><input id="wbsCode" value="${escapeHtml(selected.wbs_short_name||'')}"><label>WBS Name</label><input id="wbsName" value="${escapeHtml(selected.wbs_name||'')}"><label>Parent WBS</label><select id="wbsParent">${parentOptions}</select><label>Sequence</label><input id="wbsSeq" type="number" min="1" step="1" value="${escapeHtml(selected.seq_num||'')}"><label>OBS Responsible Manager</label><select id="wbsObs"><option value="">— Unassigned —</option>${getOBSRows(state.model).map(o=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(o.obs_id)}" ${String(o.obs_id)===String(selected.obs_id||'')?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(o.obs_name||o.obs_short_name||o.obs_id)}</option>`).join('')}</select><label>Anticipated Start</label><input id="wbsAntStart" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(String(selected.anticip_start_date||'').slice(0,10))}"><label>Anticipated Finish</label><input id="wbsAntFinish" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(String(selected.anticip_end_date||selected.anticip_finish_date||'').slice(0,10))}"><label>Estimated Weight</label><input id="wbsEstWt" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(selected.est_wt||'')}"><label>EV Compute Type</label><input id="wbsEvType" value="${escapeHtml(selected.ev_compute_type||'')}"><label>ETC Compute Type</label><input id="wbsEtcType" value="${escapeHtml(selected.ev_etc_compute_type||'')}"><label>EV User %</label><input id="wbsEvPct" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(selected.ev_user_pct||'')}"><label>WBS Notebook / Notes</label><textarea id="wbsNote" rows="4">${escapeHtml(getWBSNote(state.model,selected.wbs_id))}</textarea><label>WBS ID</label><input value="${escapeHtml(selected.wbs_id)}" disabled></div><div class="wbs-summary"><span><b>${directCount(selected.wbs_id)}</b> direct activities</span><span><b>${subtreeCount(selected.wbs_id)}</b> activities in subtree</span><span><b>${wbsDescendantIds(state.model,selected.wbs_id).length}</b> child/descendant WBS nodes</span></div></div>`:'<div class="empty">Select or add a WBS element.</div>'}
 </div>
 </div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Activities in selected WBS</h3><div class="button-row"><select id="wbsActivityTarget" ${activityRows.length?'':'disabled'}>${rows.filter(w=>!selected||w.wbs_id!==selected.wbs_id).map(w=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(w.wbs_id)}">${escapeHtml(w.wbs_short_name||w.wbs_id)} — ${escapeHtml(w.wbs_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}</select><button id="wbsMoveActivities" ${activityRows.length&&rows.length>1?'':'disabled'}>Move selected activities</button></div></div>
 <div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="width:34px"><input id="wbsCheckAll" type="checkbox"></th><th>Activity ID</th><th>Activity Name</th><th>Status</th><th>Type</th></tr></thead><tbody>${activityRows.map(t=>`<tr draggable="true" data-task-id="${escapeHtml(t.task_id)}"><td><input class="wbs-task-check" type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(t.task_id)}"></td><td>${escapeHtml(t.task_code||t.task_id)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.status_code||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.task_type||'')}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="5"><div class="empty">No activities directly assigned to this WBS.</div></td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
 $$('.wbs-tree-row').forEach(el=>{el.onclick=e=>{if(e.target.closest('[data-toggle-wbs]'))return;state.selectedWbsId=el.dataset.wbsId;renderWBS()};el.ondragstart=e=>{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/wbs-id',el.dataset.wbsId);e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move'};el.ondragover=e=>{e.preventDefault();el.classList.add('drop-target')};el.ondragleave=()=>el.classList.remove('drop-target');el.ondrop=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();el.classList.remove('drop-target');const taskIds=(e.dataTransfer.getData('text/task-ids')||'').split(',').filter(Boolean);if(taskIds.length){try{checkpoint('Drag activities to WBS');reassignTasksToWBS(state.model,taskIds,el.dataset.wbsId);state.selectedWbsId=el.dataset.wbsId;renderWBS();toast(`${taskIds.length} activities moved`)}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}return}const moving=e.dataTransfer.getData('text/wbs-id');if(!moving||moving===el.dataset.wbsId)return;try{checkpoint('Move WBS');moveWBS(state.model,moving,el.dataset.wbsId);state.selectedWbsId=moving;renderWBS();toast('WBS moved')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}}});$$('[data-toggle-wbs]').forEach(t=>t.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();const id=t.dataset.toggleWbs;state.wbsExpanded[id]=state.wbsExpanded[id]===false?true:false;renderWBS()});$$('#view tbody tr[data-task-id]').forEach(tr=>tr.ondragstart=e=>{let ids=$$('.wbs-task-check:checked').map(c=>c.value);if(!ids.includes(tr.dataset.taskId))ids=[tr.dataset.taskId];e.dataTransfer.setData('text/task-ids',ids.join(','));e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move'});
 $('#wbsAddRoot').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Add root WBS');const w=addWBS(state.model,state.projId,'',{wbs_name:'New Root WBS'});state.selectedWbsId=w.wbs_id;renderWBS();toast('Root WBS added')};
 if($('#wbsAddChild'))$('#wbsAddChild').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Add child WBS');const w=addWBS(state.model,state.projId,state.selectedWbsId,{wbs_name:'New Child WBS'});state.selectedWbsId=w.wbs_id;renderWBS();toast('Child WBS added')};
 if($('#wbsAddSibling'))$('#wbsAddSibling').onclick=()=>{const cur=state.model.find('PROJWBS','wbs_id',state.selectedWbsId);checkpoint('Add sibling WBS');const w=addWBS(state.model,state.projId,cur?.parent_wbs_id||'',{wbs_name:'New Sibling WBS'});state.selectedWbsId=w.wbs_id;renderWBS();toast('Sibling WBS added')};
 if($('#wbsCopy'))$('#wbsCopy').onclick=()=>{state.wbsClipboard=state.selectedWbsId;toast('WBS branch copied')};
 if($('#wbsPaste'))$('#wbsPaste').onclick=()=>{try{checkpoint('Paste WBS branch');const r=cloneWBSBranch(state.model,state.wbsClipboard,{newParentWbsId:state.selectedWbsId,includeActivities:true});state.selectedWbsId=r.rootWbsId;renderWBS();toast('WBS branch pasted with activities')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
 if($('#wbsDuplicate'))$('#wbsDuplicate').onclick=()=>{try{const cur=state.model.find('PROJWBS','wbs_id',state.selectedWbsId);checkpoint('Duplicate WBS branch');const r=cloneWBSBranch(state.model,state.selectedWbsId,{newParentWbsId:cur?.parent_wbs_id||'',includeActivities:true});state.selectedWbsId=r.rootWbsId;renderWBS();toast('WBS branch duplicated')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
 if($('#wbsExpandAll'))$('#wbsExpandAll').onclick=()=>{for(const w of rows)state.wbsExpanded[w.wbs_id]=true;renderWBS()};
 if($('#wbsCollapseAll'))$('#wbsCollapseAll').onclick=()=>{for(const w of rows)state.wbsExpanded[w.wbs_id]=false;renderWBS()};
 if($('#wbsSave'))$('#wbsSave').onclick=()=>{try{checkpoint('Edit WBS');ensureTable(state.model,'PROJWBS',['wbs_id','proj_id','parent_wbs_id','seq_num','wbs_short_name','wbs_name','obs_id','anticip_start_date','anticip_end_date','est_wt','ev_compute_type','ev_etc_compute_type','ev_user_pct']);updateWBS(state.model,state.selectedWbsId,{wbs_short_name:$('#wbsCode').value.trim(),wbs_name:$('#wbsName').value.trim(),parent_wbs_id:$('#wbsParent').value,seq_num:$('#wbsSeq').value||'1',obs_id:$('#wbsObs').value,anticip_start_date:$('#wbsAntStart').value,anticip_end_date:$('#wbsAntFinish').value,est_wt:$('#wbsEstWt').value,ev_compute_type:$('#wbsEvType').value,ev_etc_compute_type:$('#wbsEtcType').value,ev_user_pct:$('#wbsEvPct').value});setWBSNote(state.model,state.selectedWbsId,$('#wbsNote').value);renderWBS();toast('WBS updated')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
 if($('#wbsDelete'))$('#wbsDelete').onclick=()=>showWBSDeleteDialog(selected);
 if($('#wbsCheckAll'))$('#wbsCheckAll').onchange=e=>$$('.wbs-task-check').forEach(c=>c.checked=e.target.checked);
 if($('#wbsMoveActivities'))$('#wbsMoveActivities').onclick=()=>{const ids=$$('.wbs-task-check:checked').map(c=>c.value),target=$('#wbsActivityTarget').value;if(!ids.length)return toast('Select one or more activities');try{checkpoint('Move activities to WBS');const n=reassignTasksToWBS(state.model,ids,target);renderWBS();toast(`${n} activit${n===1?'y':'ies'} moved`)}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
}
function showWBSDeleteDialog(w){
 if(!w)return;const direct=taskRows(state.model,state.projId).filter(t=>String(t.wbs_id)===String(w.wbs_id)).length,desc=wbsDescendantIds(state.model,w.wbs_id),subtree=new Set([String(w.wbs_id),...desc]),total=taskRows(state.model,state.projId).filter(t=>subtree.has(String(t.wbs_id))).length,rows=wbsRows(state.model,state.projId),parent=String(w.parent_wbs_id||'');
 const safeTargets=rows.filter(x=>!subtree.has(String(x.wbs_id))).map(x=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(x.wbs_id)}" ${String(x.wbs_id)===parent?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(x.wbs_short_name||x.wbs_id)} — ${escapeHtml(x.wbs_name||'')}</option>`).join('');
 $('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content"><h2>Delete WBS: ${escapeHtml(w.wbs_short_name||w.wbs_id)}</h2><p>This WBS contains <b>${direct}</b> direct activities, <b>${desc.length}</b> descendant WBS nodes, and <b>${total}</b> activities in its full subtree.</p><div class="delete-choice"><label><input type="radio" name="wbsDeleteMode" value="promoteContents" ${safeTargets?'checked':''} ${safeTargets?'':'disabled'}><span><b>Remove this WBS only</b><small>Move its direct activities and child WBS nodes to the selected destination. No activities are deleted.</small></span></label><select id="wbsDeleteTarget" ${safeTargets?'':'disabled'}>${safeTargets}</select><label><input type="radio" name="wbsDeleteMode" value="deleteSubtree" ${safeTargets?'':'checked'}><span><b>Delete entire subtree</b><small>Deletes this WBS, all descendants, and all ${total} activities in the subtree, including dependent task records.</small></span></label></div><div class="button-row" style="margin-top:18px"><button id="wbsDeleteCancel">Cancel</button><button id="wbsDeleteConfirm" class="danger">Delete</button></div></div>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#wbsDeleteCancel').onclick=()=>$('#modal').close();$('#wbsDeleteConfirm').onclick=()=>{const mode=$('input[name="wbsDeleteMode"]:checked')?.value;if(!mode)return;try{checkpoint('Delete WBS');const r=deleteWBS(state.model,w.wbs_id,{mode,targetWbsId:$('#wbsDeleteTarget')?.value||null});state.selectedWbsId=wbsRows(state.model,state.projId)[0]?.wbs_id||null;$('#modal').close();renderWBS();toast(mode==='deleteSubtree'?`Deleted ${r.deletedWbs} WBS and ${r.deletedTasks} activities`:'WBS removed; contents preserved')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
}

function renderRelationships(){const rows=predRows(state.model,state.projId).map(r=>{const p=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',r.pred_task_id),s=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',r.task_id);return {...r,pred_code:p?.task_code||r.pred_task_id,pred_name:p?.task_name||'',succ_code:s?.task_code||r.task_id,succ_name:s?.task_name||''}});$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${rows.length.toLocaleString()} relationships</h3><span>Logic network records from TASKPRED</span></div>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'pred_code',label:'Predecessor'},{key:'pred_name',label:'Predecessor Name'},{key:'pred_type',label:'Type'},{key:'lag_hr_cnt',label:'Lag (h)',num:true},{key:'succ_code',label:'Successor'},{key:'succ_name',label:'Successor Name'}])}</div>`}

function renderResources(){const summary=summarizeResources(state.model,state.projId), rows=resourceSheetRows(state.model,state.projId);$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="cards">${card('Assignments',rows.length.toLocaleString())}${card('Resources',summary.length.toLocaleString())}${card('Budget Units',summary.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.budget_units,0).toFixed(1))}${card('Actual Units',summary.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.actual_units,0).toFixed(1))}${card('Remaining Units',summary.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.remaining_units,0).toFixed(1))}${card('Budget Cost',summary.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.budget_cost,0).toLocaleString())}</div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Resource summary</h3><div class="button-row"><button data-jump="resourceSheet">Open Resource Sheet</button><button data-jump="resourceProfiles">Open Histograms / Curves</button></div></div>${tableHTML(summary,[{key:'resource_name',label:'Resource'},{key:'resource_type',label:'Type'},{key:'assignments',label:'Assignments',num:true},{key:'budget_units',label:'Budget Units',num:true},{key:'actual_units',label:'Actual Units',num:true},{key:'remaining_units',label:'Remaining Units',num:true},{key:'budget_cost',label:'Budget Cost',num:true},{key:'actual_cost',label:'Actual Cost',num:true}])}</div>`;$$('[data-jump]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setView(b.dataset.jump))}

function resourceSheetColumns(){return [{key:'task_code',label:'Activity ID'},{key:'task_name',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'wbs_id',label:'WBS ID'},{key:'resource_id',label:'Resource ID'},{key:'resource_name',label:'Resource Name'},{key:'resource_type',label:'Resource Type'},{key:'role',label:'Role'},{key:'start',label:'Start'},{key:'finish',label:'Finish'},{key:'budget_units',label:'Budget Units',num:true},{key:'actual_units',label:'Actual Units',num:true},{key:'remaining_units',label:'Remaining Units',num:true},{key:'budget_cost',label:'Budget Cost',num:true},{key:'actual_cost',label:'Actual Cost',num:true},{key:'remaining_cost',label:'Remaining Cost',num:true},{key:'curve_id',label:'Curve ID'}]}
function rowsToTSV(rows,cols){const clean=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' ');return [cols.map(c=>clean(c.label)).join('\t'),...rows.map(r=>cols.map(c=>clean(r[c.key])).join('\t'))].join('\n')}
async function copyResourceSheet(rows,cols){const text=rowsToTSV(rows,cols);try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast(`Copied ${rows.length} resource rows for Excel`)}catch{const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=text;document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();document.execCommand('copy');ta.remove();toast(`Copied ${rows.length} resource rows for Excel`)}}
function renderResourceSheet(){let rows=resourceSheetRows(state.model,state.projId);const resources=[...new Map(rows.map(r=>[r.resource_id,r.resource_name])).entries()];if(state.selectedResourceId)rows=rows.filter(r=>String(r.resource_id)===String(state.selectedResourceId));const cols=resourceSheetColumns();$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Resource Sheet</h3><span>Flat assignment view designed for Excel copy/paste</span></div><div class="button-row"><select id="resourceSheetFilter"><option value="">All resources</option>${resources.map(([id,n])=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(id)}" ${String(state.selectedResourceId)===String(id)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(n||id)}</option>`).join('')}</select><button id="copyResourceSheet" class="primary">Copy as Excel TSV</button><button id="resourceSheetCsv">Download CSV</button></div></div><div class="excel-sheet">${tableHTML(rows,cols)}</div></div>`;$('#resourceSheetFilter').onchange=e=>{state.selectedResourceId=e.target.value;renderResourceSheet()};$('#copyResourceSheet').onclick=()=>copyResourceSheet(rows,cols);$('#resourceSheetCsv').onclick=()=>downloadText(`resource-sheet-${state.projId}.csv`,toCSV(rows,cols.map(c=>c.key)),'text/csv')}
function renderResourceProfiles(){const resources=state.model.table('RSRC');const rows=buildResourceTimeSeries(state.model,state.projId,{bucket:state.resourceBucket,resourceId:state.selectedResourceId,mode:state.resourceMode}),cum=cumulativeSeries(rows);$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Resource Histograms & Curves</h3><span>P6-style time-phased resource usage and cumulative curves</span></div><div class="button-row"><select id="profileResource"><option value="">All resources</option>${resources.map(r=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(r.rsrc_id)}" ${String(state.selectedResourceId)===String(r.rsrc_id)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(r.rsrc_name||r.rsrc_id)}</option>`).join('')}</select><select id="profileBucket"><option value="day" ${state.resourceBucket==='day'?'selected':''}>Daily</option><option value="week" ${state.resourceBucket==='week'?'selected':''}>Weekly</option><option value="month" ${state.resourceBucket==='month'?'selected':''}>Monthly</option></select><select id="profileMode"><option value="units" ${state.resourceMode==='units'?'selected':''}>Units</option><option value="cost" ${state.resourceMode==='cost'?'selected':''}>Cost</option></select></div></div><div class="chart-legend"><span class="legend-budget">Budget / Planned</span><span class="legend-actual">Actual</span><span class="legend-remaining">Remaining</span><span class="legend-forecast">Forecast</span></div><h4>Histogram</h4><div id="resourceHistogram" class="resource-chart"></div><h4>Cumulative Curve</h4><div id="resourceCurve" class="resource-chart"></div><details><summary>Time-phased data table</summary>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'date',label:'Period'},{key:'budget',label:'Budget',num:true},{key:'actual',label:'Actual',num:true},{key:'remaining',label:'Remaining',num:true},{key:'forecast',label:'Forecast',num:true}])}</details></div>`;renderHistogram($('#resourceHistogram'),rows,{fields:['budget','actual','remaining']});renderCurve($('#resourceCurve'),cum,{fields:['budget','actual','forecast']});$('#profileResource').onchange=e=>{state.selectedResourceId=e.target.value;renderResourceProfiles()};$('#profileBucket').onchange=e=>{state.resourceBucket=e.target.value;renderResourceProfiles()};$('#profileMode').onchange=e=>{state.resourceMode=e.target.value;renderResourceProfiles()}}


function renderCalendars(){const rows=state.model.table('CALENDAR');$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${rows.length} calendars</h3><span>Click a calendar to inspect workweek and raw calendar data</span></div>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'clndr_id',label:'ID'},{key:'clndr_name',label:'Calendar Name'},{key:'clndr_type',label:'Type'},{key:'day_hr_cnt',label:'Hours/Day',num:true},{key:'week_hr_cnt',label:'Hours/Week',num:true}],{selectId:'clndr_id'})}</div>`;$$('#view tbody tr').forEach(tr=>tr.onclick=()=>showCalendar(tr.dataset.id))}
function showCalendar(id){const c=state.model.find('CALENDAR','clndr_id',id),parsed=parseCalendarData(c?.clndr_data||'');const days=['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];$('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content"><h2>${escapeHtml(c?.clndr_name||id)}</h2><p>${escapeHtml(c?.clndr_type||'')} • ${escapeHtml(c?.day_hr_cnt||'—')} h/day • ${escapeHtml(c?.week_hr_cnt||'—')} h/week</p><div class="cal-week">${days.map(d=>{const x=parsed.days.find(z=>z.day===d);return `<div class="cal-day"><b>${d}</b>${x?.periods.length?x.periods.map(p=>`<span>${escapeHtml(p.start)} – ${escapeHtml(p.finish)}</span>`).join(''):'<span>Non-working / not decoded</span>'}</div>`}).join('')}</div><h3>Exceptions</h3><p>${parsed.exceptions.map(escapeHtml).join('<br>')||'No exceptions decoded from calendar string.'}</p><details><summary>Raw clndr_data</summary><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(c?.clndr_data||'')}</pre></details></div>`;$('#modal').showModal()}

function renderCodes(){const links=state.model.table('TASKACTV').filter(l=>state.model.find('TASK','task_id',l.task_id)?.proj_id===String(state.projId)), rows=links.map(l=>{const t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',l.task_id),c=state.model.find('ACTVCODE','actv_code_id',l.actv_code_id),ty=c?state.model.find('ACTVTYPE','actv_code_type_id',c.actv_code_type_id):null;return {task_code:t?.task_code,task_name:t?.task_name,type:ty?.actv_code_type||ty?.actv_code_type_name,code:c?.short_name||c?.actv_code_name,description:c?.actv_code_name}});const udf=state.model.table('UDFVALUE').filter(v=>state.model.find('TASK','task_id',v.fk_id)?.proj_id===String(state.projId)).map(v=>{const t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',v.fk_id),u=state.model.find('UDFTYPE','udf_type_id',v.udf_type_id);return {task_code:t?.task_code,task_name:t?.task_name,udf:u?.udf_type_label||u?.udf_type_name,value:v.udf_text||v.udf_number||v.udf_date||v.udf_code_id}});$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Activity code assignments</h3></div>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'type',label:'Code Type'},{key:'code',label:'Code'},{key:'description',label:'Description'}])}</div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>User-defined fields</h3></div>${tableHTML(udf,[{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'udf',label:'UDF'},{key:'value',label:'Value'}])}</div>`}

function renderAnalysis(){const a=analyzeSchedule(state.model,state.projId,state.settings), rules=['All',...Object.keys(a.byRule).sort()], rows=state.analysisRule==='All'?a.issues:(a.byRule[state.analysisRule]||[]);$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="cards">${card('Activities',a.counts.activities)}${card('Relationships',a.counts.relationships)}${card('Critical / ≤0',a.counts.critical)}${card('High severity',a.counts.high)}${card('Medium severity',a.counts.medium)}${card('Total findings',a.issues.length)}</div><div class="analysis-layout"><div class="panel rule-list"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Checks</h3></div><div class="panel-body">${rules.map(r=>`<button data-rule="${escapeHtml(r)}" class="${state.analysisRule===r?'active':''}">${escapeHtml(r)} <b style="float:right">${r==='All'?a.issues.length:(a.byRule[r]?.length||0)}</b></button>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${escapeHtml(state.analysisRule)} findings</h3><span>Thresholds are configurable in Settings</span></div>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'severity',label:'Severity',render:r=>`<span class="badge ${r.severity}">${escapeHtml(r.severity)}</span>`},{key:'rule',label:'Rule'},{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'detail',label:'Detail'}],{selectId:'task_id'})}</div></div>`;$$('[data-rule]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.analysisRule=b.dataset.rule;renderAnalysis()});$$('#view tbody tr[data-id]').forEach(tr=>tr.onclick=()=>{state.selectedTaskId=tr.dataset.id;setView('activities')})}

function renderBaselines(){
 if(!state.model)return;
 const b=selectedBaseline();
 if(!state.baselines.length){$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="welcome" style="margin-top:3vh"><div class="hero-icon">BL</div><h2>Project Baselines</h2><p>Load one or more approved XER or Microsoft Project XML schedules. Assign Project, Primary, Secondary or Tertiary baseline roles and compare current performance against them.</p><button id="baselineOpenInline" class="primary large">Load baseline schedule(s)</button></div>`;$('#baselineOpenInline').onclick=()=>$('#baselineInput').click();return}
 const sum=b?baselineSummary(state.model,state.projId,b,state.settings.hoursPerDay):null;
 const rows=b?baselineRows(state.model,state.projId,b,state.settings.hoursPerDay):[];
 const fmt=v=>v instanceof Date&&!isNaN(v)?fmtDate(v):'—'; const variance=v=>Number.isFinite(v)?`<span class="${v>0?'baseline-late':v<0?'baseline-ahead':''}">${v>0?'+':''}${v.toFixed(1)}d</span>`:'—';
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="baseline-manager"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Loaded baselines (${state.baselines.length})</h3><button id="addBaseline">+ Load Baseline(s)</button></div><div class="panel-body"><p class="muted">Roles mirror P6-style baseline assignment. The Primary baseline drives baseline bars and default variance columns.</p>${state.baselines.map(x=>`<div class="baseline-item ${x.id===b?.id?'selected':''}" data-id="${escapeHtml(x.id)}" style="border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:7px;padding:10px;margin:7px 0;cursor:pointer"><b>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</b><div class="sub">${escapeHtml(x.fileName||'')} • ${escapeHtml(x.importedAt.slice(0,10))}</div><div class="baseline-row-actions" style="margin-top:7px"><select class="baseline-role" data-id="${escapeHtml(x.id)}"><option ${x.role==='Unassigned'?'selected':''}>Unassigned</option><option ${x.role==='Project'?'selected':''}>Project</option><option ${x.role==='Primary'?'selected':''}>Primary</option><option ${x.role==='Secondary'?'selected':''}>Secondary</option><option ${x.role==='Tertiary'?'selected':''}>Tertiary</option></select><button class="set-primary" data-id="${escapeHtml(x.id)}">Set Primary</button><button class="remove-baseline danger" data-id="${escapeHtml(x.id)}">Remove</button></div></div>`).join('')}<label class="switch"><input id="showBaselineBars" type="checkbox" ${state.showBaselineBars?'checked':''}> Show Primary baseline bars in Gantt</label></div></div><div><div class="cards">${sum?card('Matched',`${sum.matched}/${sum.current}`,'Current activities')+card('Late vs BL',sum.late,'Finish variance > 0')+card('Ahead vs BL',sum.ahead,'Finish variance < 0')+card('Current only',sum.unmatchedCurrent,'Not in baseline')+card('Baseline only',sum.unmatchedBaseline,'Removed from current')+card('Avg Finish Var',`${sum.avgFinishVarianceDays>=0?'+':''}${sum.avgFinishVarianceDays.toFixed(1)}d`,'Positive = late'):''}</div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>${escapeHtml(b?.name||'Baseline')} → Current</h3><button id="exportBaselineVariance">Export variance CSV</button></div>${tableHTML(rows,[{key:'status',label:'Match Status'},{key:'activity',label:'Activity ID'},{key:'name',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'baseline_start',label:'BL Start',render:r=>fmt(r.baseline_start)},{key:'current_start',label:'Current Start',render:r=>fmt(r.current_start)},{key:'start_variance_days',label:'Start Var',render:r=>variance(r.start_variance_days)},{key:'baseline_finish',label:'BL Finish',render:r=>fmt(r.baseline_finish)},{key:'current_finish',label:'Current Finish',render:r=>fmt(r.current_finish)},{key:'finish_variance_days',label:'Finish Var',render:r=>variance(r.finish_variance_days)},{key:'duration_variance_days',label:'Duration Var',render:r=>variance(r.duration_variance_days)},{key:'float_variance_days',label:'Float Var',render:r=>variance(r.float_variance_days)}])}</div></div></div>`;
 $('#addBaseline').onclick=()=>$('#baselineInput').click();$('#showBaselineBars').onchange=e=>{state.showBaselineBars=e.target.checked;renderBaselines()};
 $$('.baseline-item').forEach(el=>el.onclick=e=>{if(e.target.closest('button,select'))return;state.selectedBaselineId=el.dataset.id;renderBaselines()});
 $$('.baseline-role').forEach(el=>el.onchange=e=>{setRole(state.baselines,el.dataset.id,e.target.value);if(e.target.value==='Primary')state.primaryBaselineId=el.dataset.id;renderBaselines();toast('Baseline role updated')});
 $$('.set-primary').forEach(el=>el.onclick=()=>{setRole(state.baselines,el.dataset.id,'Primary');state.primaryBaselineId=el.dataset.id;state.selectedBaselineId=el.dataset.id;renderBaselines();toast('Primary baseline assigned')});
 $$('.remove-baseline').forEach(el=>el.onclick=()=>{state.baselines=state.baselines.filter(x=>x.id!==el.dataset.id);if(state.primaryBaselineId===el.dataset.id)state.primaryBaselineId=state.baselines[0]?.id||null;if(state.selectedBaselineId===el.dataset.id)state.selectedBaselineId=state.primaryBaselineId;renderBaselines();toast('Baseline removed')});
 $('#exportBaselineVariance').onclick=()=>downloadText('baseline-variance.csv',toCSV(rows.map(r=>({...r,current_start:r.current_start?.toISOString?.()||'',current_finish:r.current_finish?.toISOString?.()||'',baseline_start:r.baseline_start?.toISOString?.()||'',baseline_finish:r.baseline_finish?.toISOString?.()||''}))),'text/csv;charset=utf-8');
}

function pickJsonFile(onFile){const i=document.createElement('input');i.type='file';i.accept='.json,application/json';i.onchange=()=>i.files?.[0]&&onFile(i.files[0]);i.click()}
function renderTutorial(){
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel" style="margin-bottom:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Unified file formats</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><p>This application opens either Primavera P6 XER or Microsoft Project XML into one canonical schedule model. You can then save the edited schedule as either format.</p><p><b>XER → XML:</b> P6 WBS becomes MSP summary tasks; activities become leaf tasks; FS/SS/FF/SF logic, lag, resources, assignments and BIM text UDFs are mapped. <b>XML → XER:</b> MSP summary tasks become P6 WBS nodes and leaf tasks become P6 activities. The export command displays a conversion audit when source-only fields cannot be mapped exactly.</p><p>Use native-format export for the greatest fidelity. Cross-format export is intended as a controlled interoperability workflow, not a promise that proprietary application-specific settings are identical.</p></div></div>`+tutorialHTML(); bindTutorial();
}
function renderBimModel(){
 const model=currentBimModel();
 const tasks=state.model?taskRows(state.model,state.projId):[];
 const date=state.bimSimDate;
 const scene=state.bimScene;
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="bim-viewer-toolbar panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Local BIM Model / 4D Viewer</h3><span>Open a local GLB and matching metadata. Geometry never leaves this machine.</span></div><div class="button-row"><button id="openGlbBtn" class="primary">Open GLB</button><button id="openMetaForViewer">Load Metadata</button><button id="fitModelBtn" ${scene?'':'disabled'}>Fit</button><button id="showLinkedBtn" ${scene&&state.model?'':'disabled'}>Show linked</button><button id="isolateLinkedBtn" ${scene&&state.model?'':'disabled'}>Isolate linked</button><button id="showAllModelBtn" ${scene?'':'disabled'}>Show all</button></div></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="button-row"><label>Simulation date <input id="simDate" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(date)}"></label><button id="apply4dBtn" ${scene&&state.model?'':'disabled'}>Apply 4D colours</button><span class="sub">${scene?`${escapeHtml(scene.name)} • ${scene.items.length.toLocaleString()} drawable primitives`:'No GLB loaded'}</span></div></div></div>
 <div class="bim-canvas-wrap"><canvas id="bimCanvas"></canvas><div id="bimOverlay" class="bim-overlay">${scene?'Left drag: orbit • Right drag: pan • Wheel: zoom • Click linked geometry to select':'Open a local GLB to begin.'}</div></div>
 <div class="bim-viewer-grid" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Selected BIM objects</h3><span>${state.bimPicked.length}</span></div><div class="panel-body">${state.bimPicked.length?`<ul class="link-list">${state.bimPicked.map(id=>`<li>${escapeHtml(id)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`:'<div class="empty">Click linked geometry or select objects from the BIM Link Manager.</div>'}</div></div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Linked activities</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><div id="pickedActivities"><div class="empty">No linked object selected.</div></div></div></div></div>`;
 function mount(){ if(!state.bimScene)return; const canvas=$('#bimCanvas'); state.bimViewer?.destroy?.(); state.bimViewer=mountBimViewer(canvas,state.bimScene,model,{onPick:hit=>{state.bimPicked=hit.objectIds; updatePickedActivities();}}); if(state.model)apply4d(); }
 function updatePickedActivities(){const box=$('#pickedActivities'); if(!box)return; if(!state.model||!state.bimPicked.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No linked object selected.</div>';return;} const hits=[]; for(const t of tasks){const links=getActivityBimLinks(state.model,t.task_id); if(links.some(l=>state.bimPicked.includes(l.objectId)))hits.push(t)} box.innerHTML=hits.length?tableHTML(hits,[{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Name'},{key:'status_code',label:'Status'}]):'<div class="empty">Selected BIM object is not linked to an activity.</div>'; box.querySelectorAll('tbody tr').forEach((tr,i)=>tr.onclick=()=>{state.selectedTaskId=hits[i].task_id;setView('activities')}); }
 function apply4d(){if(!state.bimViewer||!state.model)return; const map=[]; for(const t of tasks){for(const l of getActivityBimLinks(state.model,t.task_id)){if(model&&l.modelKey===model.modelKey)map.push([l.objectId,taskBimColor(t,state.bimSimDate)]);}} state.bimViewer.setColors(map); updatePickedActivities();}
 $('#openGlbBtn').onclick=()=>{const i=document.createElement('input');i.type='file';i.accept='.glb,model/gltf-binary';i.onchange=async()=>{if(!i.files?.[0])return;try{state.bimScene=await parseGLB(i.files[0]); state.bimViewer?.destroy?.(); state.bimViewer=null; renderBimModel();toast(`Loaded local GLB: ${i.files[0].name}`)}catch(e){alert(`Could not open GLB:\n${e.message}`)}};i.click()};
 $('#openMetaForViewer').onclick=()=>pickJsonFile(async f=>{try{const m=normalizeModelMetadata(JSON.parse(await f.text()),f.name);const ix=state.bimModels.findIndex(x=>x.modelKey===m.modelKey);if(ix>=0)state.bimModels[ix]=m;else state.bimModels.push(m);state.bimModelKey=m.modelKey;renderBimModel();toast('Metadata loaded for BIM viewer')}catch(e){alert(e.message)}});
 $('#fitModelBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>state.bimViewer?.fit());
 $('#showAllModelBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>state.bimViewer?.setVisible(null));
 $('#showLinkedBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!state.model||!model)return;const ids=[];for(const t of tasks)for(const l of getActivityBimLinks(state.model,t.task_id))if(l.modelKey===model.modelKey)ids.push(l.objectId);state.bimViewer?.setVisible(ids)});
 $('#isolateLinkedBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!state.model||!model)return;const t=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',state.selectedTaskId)||tasks[0];const ids=getActivityBimLinks(state.model,t?.task_id).filter(l=>l.modelKey===model.modelKey).map(l=>l.objectId);state.bimViewer?.setVisible(ids);state.bimViewer?.setSelection(ids)});
 $('#simDate').onchange=e=>{state.bimSimDate=e.target.value;apply4d()}; $('#apply4dBtn')?.addEventListener('click',apply4d); mount();
}

function currentBimModel(){return state.bimModels.find(m=>m.modelKey===state.bimModelKey)||state.bimModels[0]||null}
function renderBIM(){
 const tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId),task=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',state.selectedTaskId)||tasks[0];if(task)state.selectedTaskId=task.task_id;

 const q=String(state.bimObjectSearch||'').toLowerCase();const objects=(model?.objects||[]).filter(o=>!q||[o.objectId,o.name,o.category,o.level,o.system,o.sourceFile,o.path].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).slice(0,1000);
