 ${task?`<div class="kv" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Activity ID</b><span>${escapeHtml(task.task_code||task.task_id)}</span><b>WBS</b><span>${escapeHtml(state.model.find('PROJWBS','wbs_id',task.wbs_id)?.wbs_short_name||task.wbs_id||'—')}</span><b>Stored links</b><span>${links.length}</span></div>`:'<div class="empty">No activity selected.</div>'}
 </div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Local BIM model metadata</h3></div><div class="panel-body">${state.bimModels.length?`<label class="bim-label">Model<select id="bimModelSelect">${modelOptions}</select></label><div class="kv" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Model key</b><span>${escapeHtml(model?.modelKey||'')}</span><b>Version</b><span>${escapeHtml(model?.version||'—')}</span><b>Objects</b><span>${(model?.objects?.length||0).toLocaleString()}</span><b>Source</b><span>${escapeHtml(model?.sourceFile||'—')}</span></div>`:'<div class="empty">Load a model metadata JSON file generated with stable object IDs. The actual model remains on the local machine.</div>'}</div></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Objects in selected local model</h3><div class="button-row"><input id="bimObjectSearch" type="search" placeholder="Search ID, name, level, system…" value="${escapeHtml(state.bimObjectSearch||'')}"><button id="bimLinkSelected" class="primary" ${task&&model?'':'disabled'}>Link selected objects</button></div></div>
 <div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th style="width:34px"><input id="bimCheckAll" type="checkbox"></th><th>Stable Object ID</th><th>Name</th><th>Category</th><th>Level</th><th>System</th><th>Source</th></tr></thead><tbody>${objects.map(o=>`<tr><td><input class="bim-object-check" type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(o.objectId)}"></td><td class="mono">${escapeHtml(o.objectId)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(o.name||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(o.category||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(o.level||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(o.system||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(o.sourceFile||'')}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="7"><div class="empty">No local model metadata loaded, or no objects match the search.</div></td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Links stored on selected activity</h3><span>Written to UDFTYPE / UDFVALUE on XER export</span></div>${tableHTML(links.map((x,i)=>({...x,i})),[{key:'modelKey',label:'Model Key'},{key:'objectId',label:'Stable Object ID'},{key:'i',label:'Action',render:r=>`<button class="mini bim-unlink" data-model="${escapeHtml(r.modelKey)}" data-object="${escapeHtml(r.objectId)}">Unlink</button>`}])}<div class="panel-body"><div class="button-row"><input id="bimManualModel" placeholder="Model key"><input id="bimManualObject" placeholder="Stable object GUID / external ID"><button id="bimManualAdd" ${task?'':'disabled'}>Add manual link</button></div><small>Use stable source GUIDs / Revit UniqueIds / exported external IDs. Do not use transient viewer dbIds.</small></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Schedule BIM-link audit</h3><span>${all.length} linked activities</span></div>${tableHTML(all.map(a=>({...a,count:a.links.length,refs:a.links.slice(0,4).map(x=>`${x.modelKey}:${x.objectId}`).join('; ')+(a.links.length>4?' …':'')})),[{key:'taskCode',label:'Activity'},{key:'taskName',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'count',label:'Links'},{key:'refs',label:'Stored References'}])}</div>`;
 const linkedObjects=(model?.objects||[]).filter(o=>links.some(l=>l.modelKey===model?.modelKey&&l.objectId===o.objectId));
 const quantitySummary=linkedObjects.reduce((a,o)=>{for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(o.properties||{})){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&/qty|quantity|length|area|volume/i.test(k))a[k]=(a[k]||0)+n}return a},{});
 $('#view').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Advanced BIM / 4D Tools</h3><span>Rule linking, sets, quantities and model-revision reconciliation</span></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="button-row"><button id="bimBuildSets">Build Sets by Level</button><button id="bimReconcile">Reconcile New Model Revision</button></div><div class="form-grid" style="margin-top:10px"><label>Rule property<select id="bimRuleProp"><option>level</option><option>category</option><option>system</option><option>sourceFile</option></select></label><label>Rule value<input id="bimRuleValue" placeholder="e.g. L03"></label><label>Matched objects<input id="bimRuleCount" readonly value="0"></label><label>Action<button id="bimPreviewRule">Preview rule</button></label></div><h4>Saved BIM Sets</h4>${tableHTML(state.bimSets,[{key:'name',label:'Set'},{key:'property',label:'Property'},{key:'value',label:'Value'},{key:'objectIds',label:'Objects',render:r=>String(r.objectIds?.length||0)}])}<h4>Linked Quantity Summary</h4>${Object.keys(quantitySummary).length?tableHTML(Object.entries(quantitySummary).map(([metric,value])=>({metric,value})),[{key:'metric',label:'Property'},{key:'value',label:'Total'}]):'<div class="empty">No numeric quantity/length/area/volume properties found on linked objects.</div>'}<div id="bimReconResult"></div></div></div>`);
 $('#bimBuildSets').onclick=()=>{if(!model)return toast('Load model metadata first');const levels=[...new Set((model.objects||[]).map(o=>o.level).filter(Boolean))];state.bimSets=buildBimSets(model,'level',levels);renderBIM();toast(`${state.bimSets.length} BIM sets created`)};
 $('#bimPreviewRule').onclick=()=>{if(!model)return toast('Load model metadata first');const matches=matchBimRule(model,{conditions:[{property:$('#bimRuleProp').value,value:$('#bimRuleValue').value}]});$('#bimRuleCount').value=matches.length;if(matches.length&&task&&confirm(`Link ${matches.length} matched objects to ${task.task_code||task.task_id}?`)){checkpoint('Rule-based BIM link');addActivityBimLinks(state.model,state.projId,task.task_id,matches.map(o=>({modelKey:model.modelKey,objectId:o.objectId})));renderBIM()}};
 $('#bimReconcile').onclick=()=>pickJsonFile(async f=>{try{if(!model)return toast('Load current model metadata first');const next=normalizeModelMetadata(JSON.parse(await f.text()),f.name);const r=reconcileModelLinks(state.model,model,next);$('#bimReconResult').innerHTML=`<div class="cards" style="margin-top:12px">${card('Links checked',String(r.checked))}${card('Exact matches',String(r.exact))}${card('Missing',String(r.missing))}${card('New objects',String(r.newObjects))}</div>`;if(confirm(`Reconciliation complete: ${r.exact} exact, ${r.missing} missing. Replace current model metadata with the new revision?`)){const i=state.bimModels.findIndex(x=>x.modelKey===model.modelKey);state.bimModels[i>=0?i:state.bimModels.length]=next;state.bimModelKey=next.modelKey;renderBIM()}}catch(e){alert(e.message)}});
 if($('#bimTaskSelect'))$('#bimTaskSelect').onchange=e=>{state.selectedTaskId=e.target.value;renderBIM()};
 if($('#bimModelSelect'))$('#bimModelSelect').onchange=e=>{state.bimModelKey=e.target.value;renderBIM()};
 $('#bimLoadMeta').onclick=()=>pickJsonFile(async f=>{try{const m=normalizeModelMetadata(JSON.parse(await f.text()),f.name);const ix=state.bimModels.findIndex(x=>x.modelKey===m.modelKey);if(ix>=0)state.bimModels[ix]=m;else state.bimModels.push(m);state.bimModelKey=m.modelKey;renderBIM();toast(`Loaded ${m.objects.length.toLocaleString()} BIM objects`)}catch(err){alert(`Could not load BIM metadata:\n${err.message}`)}});
 $('#bimImportSide').onclick=()=>pickJsonFile(async f=>{try{const side=JSON.parse(await f.text());checkpoint('Import BIM links');const r=applyBimSidecar(state.model,state.projId,side,{replace:true});for(const raw of r.models||[]){try{const m=normalizeModelMetadata(raw,raw.sourceFile||'model.json');const ix=state.bimModels.findIndex(x=>x.modelKey===m.modelKey);if(ix>=0)state.bimModels[ix]=m;else state.bimModels.push(m)}catch{}}renderBIM();toast(`Imported ${r.linkCount} BIM links into XER UDFs`)}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(`Could not import BIM links:\n${err.message}`)}});
 $('#bimExportSide').onclick=()=>{const side=makeBimSidecar(state.model,state.projId,{models:state.bimModels});downloadText(`${currentProject()?.proj_short_name||'project'}.bimlink.json`,JSON.stringify(side,null,2),'application/json');toast('BIM link sidecar exported')};
 if($('#bimObjectSearch'))$('#bimObjectSearch').oninput=e=>{state.bimObjectSearch=e.target.value;renderBIM()};
 if($('#bimCheckAll'))$('#bimCheckAll').onchange=e=>$$('.bim-object-check').forEach(c=>c.checked=e.target.checked);
 if($('#bimLinkSelected'))$('#bimLinkSelected').onclick=()=>{const ids=$$('.bim-object-check:checked').map(c=>c.value);if(!ids.length)return toast('Select one or more BIM objects');try{checkpoint('Link BIM objects');addActivityBimLinks(state.model,state.projId,task.task_id,ids.map(objectId=>({modelKey:model.modelKey,objectId})));renderBIM();toast(`${ids.length} BIM object${ids.length===1?'':'s'} linked and stored in schedule text fields`)}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
 $$('.bim-unlink').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{try{checkpoint('Unlink BIM object');removeActivityBimLinks(state.model,state.projId,task.task_id,[{modelKey:b.dataset.model,objectId:b.dataset.object}]);renderBIM();toast('BIM object unlinked')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}});
 if($('#bimManualAdd'))$('#bimManualAdd').onclick=()=>{const modelKey=$('#bimManualModel').value.trim(),objectId=$('#bimManualObject').value.trim();if(!modelKey||!objectId)return toast('Enter both a model key and stable object ID');try{checkpoint('Add manual BIM link');addActivityBimLinks(state.model,state.projId,task.task_id,[{modelKey,objectId}]);renderBIM();toast('Manual BIM link stored in schedule text fields')}catch(err){state.history?.undo();alert(err.message)}};
}

function renderRaw(){const names=state.model.tableNames();const current=state.rawTable&&names.includes(state.rawTable)?state.rawTable:(names.includes('TASK')?'TASK':names[0]);state.rawTable=current;const rows=state.model.table(current), fields=state.model.fields(current);$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><strong>Table </strong><select id="rawSelect" class="raw-select">${names.map(n=>`<option ${n===current?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(n)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="button-row"><span>${rows.length.toLocaleString()} rows • ${fields.length} fields</span><button id="rawEditBtn">Edit table</button></div></div>${tableHTML(rows,fields.map(f=>({key:f,label:f})))}</div>`;$('#rawSelect').onchange=e=>{state.rawTable=e.target.value;renderRaw()};$('#rawEditBtn').onclick=()=>showRawEditor(current)}


function editorTaskOptions(selected=''){return taskRows(state.model,state.projId).map(t=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(t.task_id)}" ${String(t.task_id)===String(selected)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(t.task_code||t.task_id)} — ${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}
function editorWbsOptions(selected=''){return wbsRows(state.model,state.projId).map(w=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(w.wbs_id)}" ${String(w.wbs_id)===String(selected)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(w.wbs_short_name||w.wbs_id)} — ${escapeHtml(w.wbs_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}
function editorCalOptions(selected=''){return state.model.table('CALENDAR').map(c=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(c.clndr_id)}" ${String(c.clndr_id)===String(selected)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c.clndr_name||c.clndr_id)}</option>`).join('')}
function resourceOptions(selected=''){return state.model.table('RSRC').map(r=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(r.rsrc_id)}" ${String(r.rsrc_id)===String(selected)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(r.rsrc_name||r.rsrc_id)}</option>`).join('')}

function renderEditor(){
 const tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId), task=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',state.selectedTaskId)||tasks[0]; if(task)state.selectedTaskId=task.task_id;
 const rels=predRows(state.model,state.projId).map(r=>{const p=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',r.pred_task_id),s=state.model.find('TASK','task_id',r.task_id);return {...r,pred_code:p?.task_code||r.pred_task_id,succ_code:s?.task_code||r.task_id}});
 const asgs=resourceAssignments(state.model,state.projId);
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="calc-banner"><b>Editing enabled</b><span>${state.dirty?'Unsaved changes':'No unsaved changes'}</span><span>${state.lastCalc?`Last calculated finish: ${fmtDate(state.lastCalc.projectFinish,true)}`:'Not recalculated in this session'}</span><span>Undo: ${state.history?.undoStack.length||0} • Redo: ${state.history?.redoStack.length||0}</span></div>
 <div class="editor-grid" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Activity Editor</h3><div class="button-row"><button id="newTaskBtn">+ Activity</button><button id="deleteTaskBtn" class="danger" ${task?'':'disabled'}>Delete</button><button id="recalcInline" class="primary">Schedule (F9)</button></div></div><div class="panel-body">${task?activityEditForm(task):'<div class="empty">No activity selected.</div>'}</div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Select Activity</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><input id="editorSearch" type="search" placeholder="Filter activity ID or name" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin-bottom:8px"><select id="editorTaskSelect" size="18" style="width:100%;min-height:420px">${editorTaskOptions(task?.task_id)}</select></div></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Relationships</h3><button id="addRelBtn">+ Relationship</button></div>${tableHTML(rels,[{key:'task_pred_id',label:'ID'},{key:'pred_code',label:'Predecessor'},{key:'succ_code',label:'Successor'},{key:'pred_type',label:'Type'},{key:'lag_hr_cnt',label:'Lag (h)'},{key:'task_pred_id',label:'Edit',render:r=>`<button class="mini rel-edit" data-id="${escapeHtml(r.task_pred_id)}">Edit</button>`}])}</div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Resource Assignments</h3><button id="addResBtn">+ Assignment</button></div>${tableHTML(asgs,[{key:'taskrsrc_id',label:'ID'},{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'rsrc_name',label:'Resource'},{key:'target_qty',label:'Budget Units'},{key:'act_reg_qty',label:'Actual Units'},{key:'remain_qty',label:'Remaining Units'},{key:'target_cost',label:'Budget Cost'},{key:'act_reg_cost',label:'Actual Cost'},{key:'taskrsrc_id',label:'Edit',render:r=>`<button class="mini res-edit" data-id="${escapeHtml(r.taskrsrc_id)}">Edit</button>`}])}</div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Advanced XER Table Editor</h3><span>Edit any parsed table directly; use with care.</span></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="button-row"><select id="editorRawSelect" class="raw-select">${state.model.tableNames().map(n=>`<option ${n===(state.rawEditTable||'TASK')?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(n)}</option>`).join('')}</select><button id="rawEditOpen">Open table editor</button></div></div></div>`;
 if(task)bindActivityEdit(task); $('#newTaskBtn').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Add activity');const row=addTask(state.model,state.projId,{wbs_id:task?.wbs_id||wbsRows(state.model,state.projId)[0]?.wbs_id||'',clndr_id:task?.clndr_id||state.model.table('CALENDAR')[0]?.clndr_id||'',task_code:nextTaskCode() ,task_name:'New Activity'});state.selectedTaskId=row.task_id;renderEditor()};
 $('#deleteTaskBtn').onclick=()=>{if(!task||!confirm(`Delete ${task.task_code||task.task_id} and its relationships/assignments?`))return;checkpoint('Delete activity');deleteTask(state.model,task.task_id);state.selectedTaskId=taskRows(state.model,state.projId)[0]?.task_id||null;renderEditor()};
 $('#recalcInline').onclick=runSchedule; $('#editorTaskSelect').onchange=e=>{state.selectedTaskId=e.target.value;renderEditor()}; $('#editorSearch').oninput=e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();[...$('#editorTaskSelect').options].forEach(o=>o.hidden=!o.text.toLowerCase().includes(q))};
 $('#addRelBtn').onclick=()=>showRelationshipEditor(); $$('.rel-edit').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>showRelationshipEditor(b.dataset.id)); $('#addResBtn').onclick=()=>showResourceEditor(); $$('.res-edit').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>showResourceEditor(b.dataset.id));
 $('#rawEditOpen').onclick=()=>{state.rawEditTable=$('#editorRawSelect').value;showRawEditor(state.rawEditTable)};
}
function nextTaskCode(){const nums=taskRows(state.model,state.projId).map(t=>String(t.task_code||'').match(/(\d+)$/)).filter(Boolean).map(m=>+m[1]);return `A${String((nums.length?Math.max(...nums):0)+10).padStart(4,'0')}`}
function activityEditForm(t){return `<div class="edit-form">
<label>Activity ID</label><input id="eCode" value="${escapeHtml(t.task_code||'')}"><label>Activity Name</label><input id="eName" value="${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}"><label>WBS</label><select id="eWbs">${editorWbsOptions(t.wbs_id)}</select><label>Calendar</label><select id="eCal">${editorCalOptions(t.clndr_id)}</select>
<label>Status</label><select id="eStatus">${['TK_NotStart','TK_Active','TK_Complete'].map(x=>`<option ${t.status_code===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select><label>Activity Type</label><select id="eType">${['TT_Task','TT_Rsrc','TT_LOE','TT_StartMile','TT_FinMile','TT_WBS'].map(x=>`<option ${t.task_type===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select>
<label>Original Duration (h)</label><input id="eOrig" type="number" step="0.25" value="${escapeHtml(t.target_drtn_hr_cnt||t.orig_drtn_hr_cnt||0)}"><label>Remaining Duration (h)</label><input id="eRem" type="number" step="0.25" value="${escapeHtml(t.remain_drtn_hr_cnt||0)}"><label>Physical %</label><input id="ePct" type="number" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(t.phys_complete_pct||0)}">
<label>Actual Start</label><input id="eActStart" value="${escapeHtml(t.act_start_date||'')}" placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM"><label>Actual Finish</label><input id="eActFinish" value="${escapeHtml(t.act_end_date||'')}" placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM"><label>Primary Constraint</label><input id="eCstr" value="${escapeHtml(t.cstr_type||'')}" placeholder="e.g. CS_MSO"><label>Constraint Date</label><input id="eCstrDate" value="${escapeHtml(t.cstr_date||'')}" placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM">
<label>Calculated Early Start</label><input value="${escapeHtml(t.early_start_date||'')}" readonly><label>Calculated Early Finish</label><input value="${escapeHtml(t.early_end_date||'')}" readonly><label>Total Float (h)</label><input value="${escapeHtml(t.total_float_hr_cnt||'')}" readonly><label>Free Float (h)</label><input value="${escapeHtml(t.free_float_hr_cnt||'')}" readonly></div><div class="button-row" style="margin-top:12px"><button id="saveTaskBtn" class="primary">Apply Activity Changes</button><button id="goActivityBtn">View in Activities</button></div>`}
function bindActivityEdit(t){$('#saveTaskBtn').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Edit activity');updateTask(state.model,t.task_id,{task_code:$('#eCode').value,task_name:$('#eName').value,wbs_id:$('#eWbs').value,clndr_id:$('#eCal').value,status_code:$('#eStatus').value,task_type:$('#eType').value,target_drtn_hr_cnt:$('#eOrig').value,remain_drtn_hr_cnt:$('#eRem').value,phys_complete_pct:$('#ePct').value,act_start_date:$('#eActStart').value,act_end_date:$('#eActFinish').value,cstr_type:$('#eCstr').value,cstr_date:$('#eCstrDate').value});renderEditor();toast('Activity updated')};$('#goActivityBtn').onclick=()=>setView('activities')}

function showRelationshipEditor(id=null){const r=id?state.model.find('TASKPRED','task_pred_id',id):null;$('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content"><h2>${r?'Edit':'Add'} Relationship</h2><div class="edit-form"><label>Predecessor</label><select id="rPred">${editorTaskOptions(r?.pred_task_id)}</select><label>Successor</label><select id="rSucc">${editorTaskOptions(r?.task_id||state.selectedTaskId)}</select><label>Type</label><select id="rType">${['PR_FS','PR_SS','PR_FF','PR_SF'].map(x=>`<option ${r?.pred_type===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select><label>Lag (hours)</label><input id="rLag" type="number" step="0.25" value="${escapeHtml(r?.lag_hr_cnt||0)}"></div><div class="button-row" style="margin-top:14px"><button id="rSave" class="primary">Save</button>${r?'<button id="rDelete" class="danger">Delete</button>':''}</div></div>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#rSave').onclick=()=>{checkpoint(r?'Edit relationship':'Add relationship');try{if(r)updateRelationship(state.model,r.task_pred_id,{pred_task_id:$('#rPred').value,task_id:$('#rSucc').value,pred_type:$('#rType').value,lag_hr_cnt:$('#rLag').value});else addRelationship(state.model,state.projId,$('#rPred').value,$('#rSucc').value,$('#rType').value,$('#rLag').value);$('#modal').close();renderEditor();toast('Relationship saved')}catch(e){alert(e.message)}};if(r)$('#rDelete').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Delete relationship');deleteRelationship(state.model,r.task_pred_id);$('#modal').close();renderEditor()}}
function showResourceEditor(id=null){const r=id?state.model.find('TASKRSRC','taskrsrc_id',id):null;$('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content"><h2>${r?'Edit':'Add'} Resource Assignment</h2><div class="edit-form"><label>Activity</label><select id="aTask">${editorTaskOptions(r?.task_id||state.selectedTaskId)}</select><label>Resource</label><select id="aRes">${resourceOptions(r?.rsrc_id)}</select><label>Budget Units</label><input id="aBudget" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(r?.target_qty||0)}"><label>Actual Units</label><input id="aActual" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(r?.act_reg_qty||0)}"><label>Remaining Units</label><input id="aRemain" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(r?.remain_qty||0)}"><label>Budget Cost</label><input id="aBudgetCost" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(r?.target_cost||0)}"><label>Actual Cost</label><input id="aActualCost" type="number" step="0.01" value="${escapeHtml(r?.act_reg_cost||0)}"></div><div class="button-row" style="margin-top:14px"><button id="aSave" class="primary">Save</button>${r?'<button id="aDelete" class="danger">Delete</button>':''}</div></div>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#aSave').onclick=()=>{checkpoint(r?'Edit resource assignment':'Add resource assignment');const patch={task_id:$('#aTask').value,rsrc_id:$('#aRes').value,target_qty:$('#aBudget').value,act_reg_qty:$('#aActual').value,remain_qty:$('#aRemain').value,target_cost:$('#aBudgetCost').value,act_reg_cost:$('#aActualCost').value};if(r)updateResourceAssignment(state.model,r.taskrsrc_id,patch);else addResourceAssignment(state.model,patch.task_id,patch.rsrc_id,patch);$('#modal').close();renderEditor();toast('Resource assignment saved')};if(r)$('#aDelete').onclick=()=>{checkpoint('Delete resource assignment');deleteResourceAssignment(state.model,r.taskrsrc_id);$('#modal').close();renderEditor()}}

function showRawEditor(name){const table=state.model.tables.get(name),rows=table?.rows||[],fields=table?.fields||[];const max=250;$('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content wide"><h2>Advanced Editor — ${escapeHtml(name)}</h2><p>Showing ${Math.min(rows.length,max)} of ${rows.length} rows. Double-clicking isn't required: cells are directly editable, then click Save Table Changes.</p><div class="button-row"><button id="rawAdd">+ Row</button><button id="rawSave" class="primary">Save Table Changes</button></div><div class="table-wrap" style="max-height:60vh;margin-top:10px"><table class="editable-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th>${fields.map(f=>`<th>${escapeHtml(f)}</th>`).join('')}<th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(0,max).map((r,i)=>`<tr data-i="${i}"><td>${i+1}</td>${fields.map(f=>`<td contenteditable="true" data-field="${escapeHtml(f)}">${escapeHtml(r[f]??'')}</td>`).join('')}<td><button class="mini danger raw-del" data-i="${i}">×</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#rawSave').onclick=()=>{checkpoint(`Edit ${name}`);$$('.editable-table tbody tr').forEach(tr=>{const i=+tr.dataset.i;$$('td[contenteditable=true]',tr).forEach(td=>updateRow(state.model,name,i,td.dataset.field,td.textContent))});$('#modal').close();renderEditor();toast(`${name} updated`)};$('#rawAdd').onclick=()=>{checkpoint(`Add ${name} row`);addRow(state.model,name);$('#modal').close();showRawEditor(name)};$$('.raw-del').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(!confirm('Delete this raw XER row?'))return;checkpoint(`Delete ${name} row`);deleteRow(state.model,name,+b.dataset.i);$('#modal').close();showRawEditor(name)})}

function runSchedule(){if(!state.model)return;const cycles=detectCycles(state.model,state.projId);if(cycles.length){alert(`Cannot calculate: relationship cycle detected.\n\n${cycles[0].map(id=>state.model.find('TASK','task_id',id)?.task_code||id).join(' → ')}`);return}try{const pre=validateModelIntegrity(state.model,{projectId:state.projId});if(state.settings.strictValidation&&!pre.ok){alert(`Schedule calculation stopped before F9.\n\n${integritySummary(pre)}\n\nRepair the structural errors and calculate again.`);return}checkpoint('CPM recalculation');const calc=calculateCPM(state.model,state.projId,{...state.settings,dataDate:getDataDate(state.model,state.projId)});if(state.settings.strictValidation&&calc.invalidRelationships?.length){throw new Error(`${calc.invalidRelationships.length} relationship(s) reference missing activities. Repair them or disable Strict Validation in Settings.`)}runTransaction(state.model,'CPM recalculation',()=>applyCPM(state.model,state.projId,calc),{projectId:state.projId});state.lastCalc=calc;markDirty();render();const lev=calc.leveling?.conflicts?.filter(x=>x.delayHours>0).length||0;toast(`Schedule calculated • Finish ${fmtDate(calc.projectFinish,true)}${lev?` • ${lev} leveled`:''}`);if(calc.warnings?.length)console.warn('Scheduling warnings',calc.warnings)}catch(e){console.error(e);state.history?.undo();alert(actionableError(e,{operation:'Schedule calculation'}))}}
function baseName(){return (state.fileName||'schedule').replace(/\.(xer|xml)$/i,'')}
function warnConversion(target){const a=conversionAudit(state.model,target);state.lastConversionAudit=a;if(!a.warnings.length)return true;return confirm(`Conversion quality: ${a.quality}%\n\nNotes for ${formatLabel(target)}:\n\n• ${a.warnings.join('\n• ')}\n\nContinue export?`)}
function saveXER(){if(!state.model||!warnConversion('xer'))return;const check=validateModelIntegrity(state.model,{projectId:state.projId});if(!check.ok){alert(`XER export stopped because the schedule contains structural errors.\n\n${integritySummary(check)}\n\nNo file was written.`);return}const before=semanticFingerprint(state.model);downloadText(`${baseName()}-edited.xer`,exportXER(state.model),'text/plain;charset=utf-8');const after=semanticFingerprint(state.model);if(before!==after){alert('Export safety check failed: export unexpectedly changed the working model.');return}state.dirty=false;crumb();toast('Primavera P6 XER downloaded')}
function saveMSP(){if(!state.model||!warnConversion('mspxml'))return;const check=validateModelIntegrity(state.model,{projectId:state.projId});if(!check.ok){alert(`MSP XML export stopped because the schedule contains structural errors.\n\n${integritySummary(check)}\n\nNo file was written.`);return}const before=semanticFingerprint(state.model);downloadText(`${baseName()}-edited.xml`,exportMSPXML(state.model,state.projId),'application/xml;charset=utf-8');const after=semanticFingerprint(state.model);if(before!==after){alert('Export safety check failed: export unexpectedly changed the working model.');return}state.dirty=false;crumb();toast('Microsoft Project XML downloaded')}
function doUndo(){const label=state.history?.undo();if(label){state.dirty=true;state.lastCalc=null;render();toast(`Undo: ${label}`)}updateEditButtons()}
function doRedo(){const label=state.history?.redo();if(label){state.dirty=true;state.lastCalc=null;render();toast('Redo') }updateEditButtons()}

function renderSettings(){
 const o=state.settings,cals=state.model?.table('CALENDAR')||[],resources=state.model?.table('RSRC')||[];

 const exSummary=Object.entries(o.exceptionOverrides||{}).reduce((n,[,v])=>n+Object.keys(v||{}).length,0);
 $('#view').innerHTML=`

  <section class="settings-card"><h3>Logic & out-of-sequence progress</h3><label>Out-of-sequence mode<select id="sOOS"><option value="retainedLogic" ${o.outOfSequenceMode==='retainedLogic'?'selected':''}>Retained Logic</option><option value="progressOverride" ${o.outOfSequenceMode==='progressOverride'?'selected':''}>Progress Override</option><option value="actualDates" ${o.outOfSequenceMode==='actualDates'?'selected':''}>Actual Dates</option></select></label><small>Retained Logic keeps remaining predecessor logic. Progress Override allows already-started successor remaining work to continue. Actual Dates uses available actual endpoints.</small><label>Relationship lag calendar<select id="sLagCal"><option value="predecessor" ${o.relationshipLagCalendar==='predecessor'?'selected':''}>Predecessor calendar</option><option value="successor" ${o.relationshipLagCalendar==='successor'?'selected':''}>Successor calendar</option><option value="project" ${o.relationshipLagCalendar==='project'?'selected':''}>Project/default calendar</option><option value="24h" ${o.relationshipLagCalendar==='24h'?'selected':''}>24-hour calendar</option></select></label></section>
  <section class="settings-card"><h3>Progress, suspend/resume & expected finish</h3><label>Expected Finish<select id="sExpected"><option value="respect" ${o.expectedFinishMode==='respect'?'selected':''}>Respect as scheduled finish</option><option value="constrain" ${o.expectedFinishMode==='constrain'?'selected':''}>Use as finish-no-earlier-than</option><option value="ignore" ${o.expectedFinishMode==='ignore'?'selected':''}>Ignore</option></select></label><label>Suspend / Resume<select id="sSuspend"><option value="respect" ${o.suspendResumeMode==='respect'?'selected':''}>Respect resume/restart dates</option><option value="strict" ${o.suspendResumeMode==='strict'?'selected':''}>Strict — suspended activities require resume</option><option value="ignore" ${o.suspendResumeMode==='ignore'?'selected':''}>Ignore suspend/resume fields</option></select></label></section>
  <section class="settings-card"><h3>Float & backward pass</h3><label>Float completion basis<select id="sFloatMode"><option value="projectFinish" ${o.floatMode==='projectFinish'?'selected':''}>Calculated project finish</option><option value="finishBy" ${o.floatMode==='finishBy'?'selected':''}>Project Finish By date</option><option value="longestPath" ${o.floatMode==='longestPath'?'selected':''}>Longest-path style terminal finish</option></select></label><label>Finish By<input id="sFinishBy" value="${escapeHtml(o.finishBy||'')}" placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM"></label><label>Total float calculation<select id="sTFBasis"><option value="finish" ${o.totalFloatBasis==='finish'?'selected':''}>Late Finish − Early Finish</option><option value="start" ${o.totalFloatBasis==='start'?'selected':''}>Late Start − Early Start</option><option value="minimum" ${o.totalFloatBasis==='minimum'?'selected':''}>Minimum of start/finish float</option></select></label></section>

  <section class="settings-card wide"><h3>Viewer & schedule-health thresholds</h3><div class="settings-columns"><label>Hours per day<input id="sHpd" type="number" min="1" max="24" value="${o.hoursPerDay}"></label><label>Long duration threshold (days)<input id="sLong" type="number" min="1" value="${o.longDurationDays}"></label><label>High float threshold (days)<input id="sFloat" type="number" min="1" value="${o.highFloatDays}"></label><label>Positive lag threshold (days)<input id="sLag" type="number" min="0" value="${o.lagDays}"></label></div></section>
  <section class="settings-card wide"><h3>Forensic intelligence thresholds</h3><div class="settings-columns"><label>Critical float threshold (hours)<input id="sCriticalTF" type="number" value="${o.criticalFloatThresholdHours??0}"></label><label>Near-critical threshold (hours)<input id="sNearTF" type="number" min="0" value="${o.nearCriticalThresholdHours??80}"></label><label>Material date movement (days)<input id="sMatDate" type="number" min="0" step="0.5" value="${o.materialDateChangeDays??5}"></label><label>Material float movement (days)<input id="sMatFloat" type="number" min="0" step="0.5" value="${o.materialFloatChangeDays??10}"></label><label>Material lag movement (days)<input id="sMatLag" type="number" min="0" step="0.5" value="${o.materialLagChangeDays??2}"></label><label>Lookahead weeks<input id="sLookahead" type="number" min="1" max="52" value="${o.lookaheadWeeks??6}"></label><label>Max import size (MB)<input id="sMaxImport" type="number" min="1" max="2048" value="${o.maxImportFileMB??500}"></label><label>Stalled progress threshold (%)<input id="sStallPct" type="number" min="1" max="99" value="${o.stalledThresholdPercent??90}"></label><label>Stalled periods<input id="sStallPeriods" type="number" min="2" max="24" value="${o.stalledPeriods??3}"></label><label style="grid-column:span 2">Nominated milestone IDs (comma-separated)<input id="sMilestones" value="${escapeHtml(o.nominatedMilestones||'')}"></label></div></section>
 </div>`;
 const set=(id,key,coerce=v=>v)=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.onchange=e=>{state.settings[key]=coerce(e.target.type==='checkbox'?e.target.checked:e.target.value);persistSettings();}};
 if($('#sDataDate'))$('#sDataDate').onchange=e=>{if(!state.model)return;checkpoint('Change data date');setProjectDataDate(state.model,state.projId,e.target.value);crumb();toast('Data date updated')};
 set('#sOOS','outOfSequenceMode');set('#sLagCal','relationshipLagCalendar');set('#sExpected','expectedFinishMode');set('#sSuspend','suspendResumeMode');set('#sFloatMode','floatMode');set('#sFinishBy','finishBy');set('#sTFBasis','totalFloatBasis');set('#sExternal','externalRelationshipMode');set('#sExceptionMode','calendarExceptionMode');set('#sShiftBoundary','shiftBoundaryMode');set('#sLevel','resourceLevelingEnabled',Boolean);set('#sPriority','resourcePriorityField');set('#sGran','levelingGranularityHours',Number);set('#sCurves','resourceCurveMode');set('#sStrict','strictValidation',Boolean);set('#sHpd','hoursPerDay',Number);set('#sLong','longDurationDays',Number);set('#sFloat','highFloatDays',Number);set('#sLag','lagDays',Number);set('#sCriticalTF','criticalFloatThresholdHours',Number);set('#sNearTF','nearCriticalThresholdHours',Number);set('#sMatDate','materialDateChangeDays',Number);set('#sMatFloat','materialFloatChangeDays',Number);set('#sMatLag','materialLagChangeDays',Number);set('#sLookahead','lookaheadWeeks',Number);set('#sMaxImport','maxImportFileMB',Number);set('#sStallPct','stalledThresholdPercent',Number);set('#sStallPeriods','stalledPeriods',Number);set('#sMilestones','nominatedMilestones');
 $('#sProfile').onchange=e=>{state.settings={...state.settings,...applyProfile(state.settings,e.target.value)};persistSettings();renderSettings();toast('Compatibility profile applied')};
 $$('.capacity-input').forEach(el=>el.onchange=e=>{const id=e.target.dataset.rsrc,v=Number(e.target.value);state.settings.resourceCapacityOverrides={...(state.settings.resourceCapacityOverrides||{})};if(v>0)state.settings.resourceCapacityOverrides[id]=v;else delete state.settings.resourceCapacityOverrides[id];persistSettings()});
 $('#settingsReset').onclick=()=>{state.settings={hoursPerDay:8,longDurationDays:44,highFloatDays:44,lagDays:0,criticalFloatThresholdHours:0,nearCriticalThresholdHours:80,materialDateChangeDays:5,materialFloatChangeDays:10,materialLagChangeDays:2,lookaheadWeeks:6,maxImportFileMB:500,stalledThresholdPercent:90,stalledPeriods:3,nominatedMilestones:'',...normalizeSchedulingOptions(defaultSchedulingOptions)};persistSettings();renderSettings();toast('Scheduling settings reset')};
 $('#settingsExport').onclick=()=>downloadText('p6-calculation-settings.json',JSON.stringify(state.settings,null,2),'application/json');
 $('#settingsImport').onclick=()=>$('#settingsFile').click();$('#settingsFile').onchange=async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;try{state.settings={...state.settings,...normalizeSchedulingOptions(JSON.parse(await f.text()))};persistSettings();renderSettings();toast('Settings imported')}catch{alert('Invalid settings JSON')}};
 $('#manageExceptions').onclick=showExceptionManager;
}
function showExceptionManager(){const cals=state.model?.table('CALENDAR')||[];if(!cals.length)return toast('No calendars loaded');const rows=[];for(const [cid,dates] of Object.entries(state.settings.exceptionOverrides||{}))for(const [date,periods] of Object.entries(dates||{}))rows.push({cid,date,periods});$('#modalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-content"><h2>Calendar Exception Overrides</h2><p>Add a date-specific override. Leave shifts blank to make the date non-working. Use 24-hour times, e.g. <code>08:00-12:00,13:00-17:00</code>.</p><div class="form-grid"><label>Calendar<select id="exCal">${cals.map(c=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(c.clndr_id)}">${escapeHtml(c.clndr_name||c.clndr_id)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Date<input id="exDate" type="date"></label><label style="grid-column:1/-1">Working shifts<input id="exShifts" placeholder="08:00-12:00,13:00-17:00"></label></div><button id="exAdd" class="primary">Add / Replace Exception</button><div class="table-wrap" style="margin-top:12px"><table><thead><tr><th>Calendar</th><th>Date</th><th>Shifts</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((r,i)=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(cals.find(c=>c.clndr_id===r.cid)?.clndr_name||r.cid)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.date)}</td><td>${r.periods.length?r.periods.map(x=>`${String(Math.floor(x[0]/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(x[0]%60).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(Math.floor(x[1]/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(x[1]%60).padStart(2,'0')}`).join(', '):'<em>Non-working</em>'}</td><td><button class="mini danger ex-del" data-cid="${escapeHtml(r.cid)}" data-date="${escapeHtml(r.date)}">×</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#exAdd').onclick=()=>{const cid=$('#exCal').value,date=$('#exDate').value;if(!date)return alert('Choose a date.');const raw=$('#exShifts').value.trim(),periods=[];if(raw){for(const bit of raw.split(',')){const m=bit.trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})-(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!m)return alert(`Invalid shift: ${bit}`);const a=+m[1]*60 + +m[2],b=+m[3]*60 + +m[4];if(b<=a)return alert('Shift finish must be after start.');periods.push([a,b])}}state.settings.exceptionOverrides={...(state.settings.exceptionOverrides||{}),[cid]:{...(state.settings.exceptionOverrides?.[cid]||{}),[date]:periods}};persistSettings();$('#modal').close();renderSettings();toast('Calendar exception saved')};$$('.ex-del').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const x={...(state.settings.exceptionOverrides||{})},d={...(x[b.dataset.cid]||{})};delete d[b.dataset.date];if(Object.keys(d).length)x[b.dataset.cid]=d;else delete x[b.dataset.cid];state.settings.exceptionOverrides=x;persistSettings();$('#modal').close();renderSettings();toast('Calendar exception removed')})}



function renderLayouts(){
 const layout=state.layouts.find(x=>x.id===state.activeLayoutId)||defaultLayout();const tasks=filterTasks();const built=builtInFilters();
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="cards">${card('Visible activities',tasks.length.toLocaleString(),'After active filters')}${card('Saved layouts',state.layouts.length.toLocaleString(),'Stored in this browser')}${card('Filter rules',(state.filterGroup.rules||[]).length.toString(),'AND/OR rule engine')}${card('Canonical schema',canonicalSummary(state.model).schemaVersion,'Format-neutral core')}</div>
 <div class="split"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Saved Layouts</h3><button id="newLayout">+ Layout</button></div><div class="panel-body">${state.layouts.map(l=>`<div class="baseline-item ${l.id===state.activeLayoutId?'selected':''}" data-layout="${escapeHtml(l.id)}"><b>${escapeHtml(l.name)}</b><div class="sub">Group: ${escapeHtml(l.groupBy||'None')} • Timescale: ${escapeHtml(l.timescale||'week')} • ${l.columns.length} columns</div><div class="button-row"><button class="use-layout" data-id="${escapeHtml(l.id)}">Use</button><button class="delete-layout danger" data-id="${escapeHtml(l.id)}" ${l.id==='default'?'disabled':''}>Delete</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Layout Definition</h3><button id="saveLayout" class="primary">Save layout</button></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="form-grid"><label>Name<input id="layoutName" value="${escapeHtml(layout.name)}"></label><label>Group By<select id="layoutGroup">${[['','None'],['wbs_id','WBS Description'],['status_code','Status'],['clndr_id','Calendar'],['task_type','Activity Type']].map(([x,label])=>`<option value="${x}" ${layout.groupBy===x?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Timescale<select id="layoutTimescale">${['day','week','month','quarter'].map(x=>`<option ${layout.timescale===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Row Height<select id="layoutRowHeight">${['compact','normal','large'].map(x=>`<option ${layout.rowHeight===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label style="grid-column:1/-1">Columns<input id="layoutColumns" value="${escapeHtml(layout.columns.join(', '))}"></label><label>Sort field<input id="layoutSort" value="${escapeHtml(layout.sort?.[0]?.field||'task_code')}"></label><label>Sort direction<select id="layoutDir"><option value="1" ${(layout.sort?.[0]?.dir||1)>0?'selected':''}>Ascending</option><option value="-1" ${(layout.sort?.[0]?.dir||1)<0?'selected':''}>Descending</option></select></label></div></div></div></div>
 <div class="split" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>P6-style Filters</h3><button id="clearFilters">Clear</button></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="button-row">${built.map(f=>`<button class="builtin-filter" data-id="${f.id}">${escapeHtml(f.name)}</button>`).join('')}</div><hr><div class="form-grid"><label>Field<select id="filterField">${['total_float_hr_cnt','status_code','task_type','wbs_id','remain_drtn_hr_cnt','task_name','task_code'].map(x=>`<option>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Operator<select id="filterOp">${['eq','neq','contains','gt','gte','lt','lte','blank','notblank'].map(x=>`<option>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Value<input id="filterValue"></label><label>Group mode<select id="filterMode"><option ${state.filterGroup.mode==='AND'?'selected':''}>AND</option><option ${state.filterGroup.mode==='OR'?'selected':''}>OR</option></select></label></div><button id="addFilter" class="primary">Add Rule</button><div style="margin-top:10px">${(state.filterGroup.rules||[]).map((r,i)=>`<div class="issue-row"><code>${escapeHtml(r.field)} ${escapeHtml(r.op)} ${escapeHtml(r.value??'')}</code><button class="mini remove-filter" data-i="${i}">×</button></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No active filter rules.</div>'}</div></div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Preview</h3><button id="openFilteredActivities">Open Activities</button></div>${tableHTML(tasks.slice(0,100),[{key:'task_code',label:'ID'},{key:'task_name',label:'Name'},{key:'status_code',label:'Status'},{key:'target_start_date',label:'Start'},{key:'target_end_date',label:'Finish'},{key:'total_float_hr_cnt',label:'TF (h)'}])}</div></div>`;
 $$('.use-layout').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.activeLayoutId=b.dataset.id;renderLayouts()});$$('.delete-layout').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.layouts=removeLayout(state.layouts,b.dataset.id);saveLayouts(state.layouts);state.activeLayoutId='default';renderLayouts()});
 $('#newLayout').onclick=()=>{const id=`layout-${Date.now()}`;state.layouts.push({...defaultLayout(),id,name:'New Layout'});state.activeLayoutId=id;saveLayouts(state.layouts);renderLayouts()};
 $('#saveLayout').onclick=()=>{const next={...layout,name:$('#layoutName').value||'Layout',groupBy:$('#layoutGroup').value,timescale:$('#layoutTimescale').value,rowHeight:$('#layoutRowHeight').value,columns:$('#layoutColumns').value.split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean),sort:[{field:$('#layoutSort').value.trim()||'task_code',dir:Number($('#layoutDir').value)||1}]};upsertLayout(state.layouts,next);saveLayouts(state.layouts);state.audit.add('Save layout',{name:next.name});renderLayouts();toast('Layout saved')};
 $('#addFilter').onclick=()=>{state.filterGroup.mode=$('#filterMode').value;state.filterGroup.rules.push({field:$('#filterField').value,op:$('#filterOp').value,value:$('#filterValue').value});renderLayouts()};$('#clearFilters').onclick=()=>{state.filterGroup={mode:'AND',rules:[]};renderLayouts()};$$('.remove-filter').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.filterGroup.rules.splice(+b.dataset.i,1);renderLayouts()});
 $$('.builtin-filter').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const f=built.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.id);if(f?.group)state.filterGroup=structuredClone(f.group);else if(f?.special==='openStart'){const succ=new Set(predRows(state.model,state.projId).map(r=>String(r.task_id)));state.filterGroup={mode:'OR',rules:taskRows(state.model,state.projId).filter(t=>!succ.has(String(t.task_id))).slice(0,200).map(t=>({field:'task_id',op:'eq',value:t.task_id}))}}renderLayouts()});$('#openFilteredActivities').onclick=()=>setView('activities');
}

function renderDiagnostics(){
 const tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId),target=state.selectedFloatTarget||state.selectedTaskId||tasks.at(-1)?.task_id;if(target)state.selectedFloatTarget=target;const diag=explainActivity(state.model,state.projId,target,state.lastCalc);const paths=traceFloatPaths(state.model,state.projId,target,{maxPaths:8});
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Calculation Diagnostics</h3><div class="button-row"><label>Activity <select id="diagTask">${tasks.map(t=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(t.task_id)}" ${String(t.task_id)===String(target)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(t.task_code||t.task_id)} — ${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button id="diagF9" class="primary">Schedule & Diagnose</button></div></div><div class="panel-body">${diag?`<div class="cards">${card('Early Start',fmtDate(diag.earlyStart,true))}${card('Early Finish',fmtDate(diag.earlyFinish,true))}${card('Total Float',`${diag.totalFloat} h`)}${card('Calendar',diag.calendar||'—')}</div><p><b>Likely driver:</b> ${escapeHtml(driverText(diag))}</p><div class="kv"><b>Constraints</b><span>${escapeHtml(diag.constraints.join('; ')||'None')}</span><b>Incoming logic</b><span>${diag.incoming.length}</span></div>${tableHTML(diag.incoming,[{key:'predId',label:'Pred'},{key:'predName',label:'Name'},{key:'type',label:'Type'},{key:'lagHours',label:'Lag (h)'},{key:'predStart',label:'Pred Start'},{key:'predFinish',label:'Pred Finish'}])}`:'<div class="empty">Select an activity.</div>'}</div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Multiple Float Paths</h3><span>Ranked predecessor paths to the selected target</span></div>${paths.length?`<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Path</th><th>Min Float (h)</th><th>Activities</th></tr></thead><tbody>${paths.map(p=>`<tr><td>${p.rank}</td><td>${p.floatHours}</td><td>${p.activities.map(a=>escapeHtml(a.task_code||a.task_id)).join(' → ')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="empty">No upstream path found.</div>'}</div>`;
 $('#diagTask').onchange=e=>{state.selectedFloatTarget=e.target.value;renderDiagnostics()};$('#diagF9').onclick=()=>{runSchedule();setTimeout(renderDiagnostics,0)};
}

 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>EVM Summary</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="kv"><b>BAC</b><span>${f(sum.bac)}</span><b>VAC</b><span>${f(sum.vac)}</span><b>TCPI</b><span>${f(sum.tcpi)}</span><b>Earned/Actual efficiency</b><span>${f(ps.efficiency)}</span><b>Remaining units</b><span>${f(ps.remaining)}</span></div></div></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Activity EVM</h3><button id="evmCsv">Export EVM CSV</button></div>${tableHTML(evm.slice(0,1000),[{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Name'},{key:'bac',label:'BAC'},{key:'pv',label:'PV'},{key:'ev',label:'EV'},{key:'ac',label:'AC'},{key:'spi',label:'SPI',render:r=>f(r.spi)},{key:'cpi',label:'CPI',render:r=>f(r.cpi)},{key:'eac',label:'EAC'},{key:'vac',label:'VAC'}])}</div>`;

}

function snapshotToModel(snap){const tables=new Map((snap.payload.tables||[]).map(([n,t])=>[n,{name:t.name||n,fields:[...(t.fields||[])],rows:(t.rows||[]).map(r=>({...r}))}]));const m=new XERModel({header:snap.payload.header||[],tables,warnings:[],sourceText:''});m.sourceFormat=snap.payload.sourceFormat||state.sourceFormat;return m}
function renderHistory(){
 const selectedScenario=state.scenarios[0]||null,diff=selectedScenario?scenarioTaskDiff(state.model,selectedScenario):[];
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="split"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Revision History</h3><button id="makeSnapshot" class="primary">Create Snapshot</button></div><div class="panel-body"><p>Local snapshots create a lightweight schedule revision history. Use them before F9, baseline updates, conversions or major edits.</p>${state.revisionSnapshots.map((r,i)=>`<div class="baseline-item"><b>${escapeHtml(r.label)}</b><div class="sub">${escapeHtml(r.at)}</div><div class="button-row"><button class="compare-rev" data-i="${i}">Compare</button></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No snapshots.</div>'}</div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Scenario Planning</h3><button id="newScenario">Clone Current Scenario</button></div><div class="panel-body">${state.scenarios.map((sc,i)=>`<div class="baseline-item"><b>${escapeHtml(sc.name)}</b><div class="sub">${escapeHtml(sc.createdAt)}</div><div class="button-row"><button class="promote-scenario" data-i="${i}">Promote</button><button class="remove-scenario danger" data-i="${i}">Delete</button></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No scenarios. Clone the current schedule to test recovery options without losing the live version.</div>'}</div></div></div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Scenario Difference Preview</h3><span>${diff.length} changed fields vs first saved scenario</span></div>${tableHTML(diff.slice(0,500),[{key:'task_code',label:'Activity'},{key:'task_name',label:'Name'},{key:'field',label:'Field'},{key:'scenario',label:'Scenario'},{key:'current',label:'Current'}])}</div>
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Audit Log</h3><button id="auditExport">Export JSON</button></div>${tableHTML(state.audit.items.slice(0,250).map(x=>({at:x.at,action:x.action,details:JSON.stringify(x.details||{})})),[{key:'at',label:'Time'},{key:'action',label:'Action'},{key:'details',label:'Details'}])}</div>`;
 $('#makeSnapshot').onclick=()=>{state.revisionSnapshots.push(snapshotModel(state.model,`Snapshot ${state.revisionSnapshots.length+1}`));state.audit.add('Create revision snapshot');renderHistory();toast('Revision snapshot created')};$$('.compare-rev').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const snap=state.revisionSnapshots[+b.dataset.i];state.compareModel=snapshotToModel(snap);state.compareProjId=state.projId;setView('compare')});$('#newScenario').onclick=()=>{state.scenarios.unshift(createScenario(state.model,`Scenario ${state.scenarios.length+1}`));state.audit.add('Create scenario',{name:state.scenarios[0].name});renderHistory();toast('Scenario cloned')};$$('.promote-scenario').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(!confirm('Promote this scenario over the current schedule?'))return;checkpoint('Promote scenario');promoteScenario(state.model,state.scenarios[+b.dataset.i]);state.history=new EditHistory(state.model);renderHistory();toast('Scenario promoted')});$$('.remove-scenario').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.scenarios.splice(+b.dataset.i,1);renderHistory()});$('#auditExport').onclick=()=>downloadText('schedule-audit-log.json',state.audit.export(),'application/json');
}

 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="cards">${card('Canonical rows',cs.total.toLocaleString(),`Schema v${cs.schemaVersion}`)}${card('Local persistence',state.autosaveEnabled?'Enabled':'Disabled','IndexedDB')}${card('Package size',`${(packageSize/1024).toFixed(1)} KB`,'Portable .ussproj JSON')}${card('Calendars',cals.length.toString(),'Visual/exception validation')}</div>
 <div class="split"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Local Project Workspace</h3><div class="button-row"><button id="workspaceSave" class="primary">Save Locally</button><button id="workspaceRefresh">Refresh Projects</button></div></div><div class="panel-body"><label class="switch"><input id="autosaveToggle" type="checkbox" ${state.autosaveEnabled?'checked':''}> Autosave after edits</label><div id="workspaceList"><div class="empty">Click Refresh Projects to list locally saved workspaces.</div></div><hr><div class="button-row"><button id="pkgSave">Download Project Package</button><button id="pkgOpen">Open Project Package</button></div><small>The package stores schedule tables, settings, baselines, layouts, BIM metadata, scenarios and audit history in one portable file.</small></div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Cross-format Portability Audit</h3></div>${tableHTML(portable,[{key:'feature',label:'Feature'},{key:'xer',label:'P6 XER'},{key:'mspxml',label:'MSP XML'}])}</div></div>

 $('#workspaceSave').onclick=()=>saveLocalWorkspace(false);$('#workspaceRefresh').onclick=async()=>{try{const items=await listWorkspaces();$('#workspaceList').innerHTML=items.map(x=>`<div class="baseline-item"><b>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</b><div class="sub">${escapeHtml(x.updatedAt)}</div><button class="open-ws" data-id="${escapeHtml(x.id)}">Open</button></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No locally saved projects.</div>';$$('.open-ws').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{const w=await getWorkspace(b.dataset.id);if(w?.package){const pkg=w.package;state.model=modelFromPackage(pkg);state.sourceFormat=state.model.sourceFormat;state.settings={...state.settings,...(pkg.settings||{})};state.baselines=(pkg.baselines||[]).map(b=>{if(b.model)return b;const bm=new XERModel({header:[],tables:new Map((b.tables||[]).map(([n,t])=>[n,{name:t.name||n,fields:[...(t.fields||[])],rows:(t.rows||[]).map(r=>({...r}))}])),warnings:[],sourceText:''});bm.sourceFormat=b.format||'xer';return {...b,model:bm}});state.primaryBaselineId=state.baselines.find(b=>b.role==='Primary')?.id||null;state.selectedBaselineId=state.primaryBaselineId||state.baselines[0]?.id||null;state.bimModels=pkg.bimModels||[];state.layouts=pkg.layouts||[defaultLayout()];state.scenarios=pkg.scenarios||[];state.projId=state.model.table('PROJECT')[0]?.proj_id||state.model.table('TASK')[0]?.proj_id||null;state.revisionRepository=pkg.revisions?.length?pkg.revisions:[createRevision(state.model,{name:'Package opened',projectId:state.projId})];state.selectedRevisionA=state.revisionRepository[0]?.id||'';state.selectedRevisionB=state.revisionRepository.at(-1)?.id||'';state.audit.items=pkg.audit||[];state.history=new EditHistory(state.model);state.workspaceId=w.id;populateProjects();renderWorkspace();toast('Local workspace opened')}})}catch(e){toast('IndexedDB workspace unavailable')}};$('#autosaveToggle').onchange=e=>state.autosaveEnabled=e.target.checked;$('#pkgSave').onclick=saveProjectPackage;$('#pkgOpen').onclick=()=>$('#packageInput').click();$('#addNthHoliday').onclick=()=>{const cal=cals[0];if(!cal)return toast('No calendars');const month=Math.max(1,Math.min(12,Number(prompt('Month (1-12)','12'))||12))-1,weekday=Math.max(0,Math.min(6,Number(prompt('Weekday (0=Sun, 1=Mon ... 6=Sat)','1'))||1)),nth=Math.max(1,Math.min(5,Number(prompt('Occurrence in month (1-5)','4'))||4)),startYear=Number(prompt('First year',String(new Date().getFullYear())))||new Date().getFullYear(),years=Math.max(1,Math.min(30,Number(prompt('How many years?','10'))||10));const overrides={...(state.settings.exceptionOverrides||{}),[cal.id]:{...(state.settings.exceptionOverrides?.[cal.id]||{})}};let n=0;for(let y=startYear;y<startYear+years;y++){const d=recurringHolidayDates(y,month,weekday,nth);if(d){overrides[cal.id][d]=[];n++}}state.settings.exceptionOverrides=overrides;persistSettings();toast(`Added ${n} recurring non-working dates to ${cal.name}`)};
}

function revisionById(id){return state.revisionRepository.find(r=>r.id===id)||null}
function revisionOptions(selected=''){return state.revisionRepository.map(r=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(r.id)}" ${r.id===selected?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(r.name)} • ${escapeHtml(r.dataDate||'no data date')}</option>`).join('')}
function storeCurrentRevision(){if(!state.model)return;const name=prompt('Revision name',`Revision ${state.revisionRepository.length+1}`);if(!name)return;const description=prompt('Optional description','')||'';const r=createRevision(state.model,{name,description,projectId:state.projId,sourceFile:state.fileName});state.revisionRepository.push(r);state.selectedRevisionB=r.id;if(!state.selectedRevisionA)state.selectedRevisionA=state.revisionRepository[0]?.id||'';state.audit.add('Store forensic revision',{name,projectId:state.projId});markDirty();renderIntelligence();toast('Revision stored')}
function addLoadedCompareToRepository(){if(!state.compareModel)return toast('Load a comparison schedule first');const name=prompt('Revision name for the loaded comparison schedule','Previous Revision');if(!name)return;const r=createRevision(state.compareModel,{name,projectId:state.compareProjId,sourceFile:'comparison schedule'});state.revisionRepository.unshift(r);state.selectedRevisionA=r.id;state.selectedRevisionB=state.revisionRepository.find(x=>x!==r)?.id||state.revisionRepository.at(-1)?.id||'';renderIntelligence();toast('Comparison schedule added to revision repository')}
function runForensicComparison(){const a=revisionById(state.selectedRevisionA),b=revisionById(state.selectedRevisionB);if(!a||!b)return toast('Choose two revisions');const timer=logger.start('forensicCompare',{from:a.name,to:b.name});const oldM=revisionModel(a),newM=revisionModel(b);state.forensicComparison=materialChangeRegister(oldM,newM,state.settings);state.forensicComparison.migration=criticalPathMigration(oldM,newM,a.projectId,b.projectId,{thresholdHours:Number(state.settings.criticalFloatThresholdHours||0)});state.audit.add('Compare forensic revisions',{from:a.name,to:b.name,changes:state.forensicComparison.changes.length});logger.end('forensicCompare','app','runForensicComparison','Forensic comparison complete');renderIntelligence()}
function renderIntelligence(){
 const currentCP=criticalPathReport(state.model,state.projId,{thresholdHours:Number(state.settings.criticalFloatThresholdHours||0),targetTaskId:state.selectedTaskId});
 const c=state.forensicComparison;
 $('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="cards">${card('Stored revisions',state.revisionRepository.length)}${card('Current critical activities',currentCP.rows.length)}${card('Material changes',c?.summary?.material||0)}${card('Critical changes',c?.summary?.critical||0)}</div>
 <div class="split" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h3>Revision Repository</h3><span>Named full-model snapshots for forensic comparison and trends</span></div><div class="button-row"><button id="storeRevision" class="primary">Store Current Revision</button><button id="addCompareRevision">Add Loaded Compare</button><button id="exportRepo">Backup Repository</button><button id="importRepo">Restore Repository</button><input id="repoInput" type="file" accept=".json" hidden></div></div><div class="panel-body">${state.revisionRepository.map(r=>`<div class="baseline-item"><b>${escapeHtml(r.name)}</b><div class="sub">${escapeHtml(r.dataDate||'No data date')} • ${escapeHtml(r.projectName||r.projectId||'')} • ${escapeHtml(r.description||'')}</div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No stored revisions.</div>'}</div></div>
 <div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Compare Revisions</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><label>Previous</label><select id="forensicA"><option value="">Select…</option>${revisionOptions(state.selectedRevisionA)}</select><label>Current</label><select id="forensicB"><option value="">Select…</option>${revisionOptions(state.selectedRevisionB)}</select><div class="button-row" style="margin-top:10px"><button id="runForensic" class="primary">Run Full Comparison</button></div>${c?`<p><b>${c.changes.length.toLocaleString()}</b> underlying field/record changes; <b>${c.summary.material||0}</b> material; <b>${c.summary.critical||0}</b> critical.</p>`:'<p class="sub">Comparison includes all canonical XER tables, not only visible activity dates.</p>'}</div></div></div>
 ${c?`<div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Material Change Register</h3><button id="downloadChangeRegister">Download CSV</button></div>${tableHTML(c.changes.slice(0,1500),[{key:'criticality',label:'Criticality'},{key:'table',label:'Table'},{key:'activity',label:'Activity'},{key:'activityName',label:'Activity Name'},{key:'field',label:'Field'},{key:'oldValue',label:'Previous'},{key:'newValue',label:'Current'},{key:'scheduleImpact',label:'Assessment'}])}</div>
 <div class="split" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Critical Path Migration</h3></div>${tableHTML([...(c.migration?.entered||[]).map(x=>({...x,movement:'Entered critical'})),...(c.migration?.left||[]).map(x=>({...x,movement:'Left critical'}))],[{key:'movement',label:'Migration'},{key:'activity',label:'Activity'},{key:'name',label:'Name'},{key:'previousFloat',label:'Previous TF'},{key:'currentFloat',label:'Current TF'}])}</div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Why Did My Date Move?</h3></div><div class="panel-body"><select id="dateMoveTask">${taskRows(state.model,state.projId).map(t=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(t.task_code||t.task_id)}" ${(t.task_id===state.selectedTaskId||t.task_code===state.selectedTrendTask)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(t.task_code||t.task_id)} — ${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}</select><button id="runDateMove" class="primary" style="margin-left:8px">Explain</button><div id="dateMoveResult" style="margin-top:12px"></div></div></div></div>`:''}
 <div class="panel" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="panel-head"><h3>Current Critical / Driving Path Evidence</h3><span>Threshold ${Number(state.settings.criticalFloatThresholdHours||0)} h</span></div>${tableHTML(currentCP.rows.slice(0,1000),[{key:'activity',label:'Activity'},{key:'name',label:'Name'},{key:'start',label:'Early Start'},{key:'finish',label:'Early Finish'},{key:'total_float_hours',label:'Total Float h',num:true},{key:'status',label:'Status'}])}</div>`;
 $('#storeRevision').onclick=storeCurrentRevision;$('#addCompareRevision').onclick=addLoadedCompareToRepository;$('#forensicA').onchange=e=>state.selectedRevisionA=e.target.value;$('#forensicB').onchange=e=>state.selectedRevisionB=e.target.value;$('#runForensic').onclick=runForensicComparison;$('#exportRepo').onclick=()=>downloadText('schedule-revision-repository.json',serializeRepository(state.revisionRepository),'application/json');$('#importRepo').onclick=()=>$('#repoInput').click();$('#repoInput').onchange=async e=>{try{state.revisionRepository=parseRepository(await e.target.files[0].text());state.selectedRevisionA=state.revisionRepository[0]?.id||'';state.selectedRevisionB=state.revisionRepository.at(-1)?.id||'';renderIntelligence();toast('Revision repository restored')}catch(err){alert(err.message)}};
 if(c){$('#downloadChangeRegister').onclick=()=>downloadText('material-change-register.csv',toCSV(c.changes),'text/csv');$('#runDateMove').onclick=()=>{const a=revisionById(state.selectedRevisionA),b=revisionById(state.selectedRevisionB),key=$('#dateMoveTask').value;if(!a||!b)return;state.selectedTrendTask=key;state.dateMoveResult=explainDateMovement(revisionModel(a),revisionModel(b),key,{oldProjId:a.projectId,newProjId:b.projectId,hoursPerDay:state.settings.hoursPerDay});const x=state.dateMoveResult;$('#dateMoveResult').innerHTML=x.available?`<p><b>${escapeHtml(x.statement)}</b></p>${tableHTML(x.contributors,[{key:'confidence',label:'Confidence'},{key:'cause',label:'Cause'},{key:'impactDays',label:'Indicative days',num:true},{key:'evidence',label:'Evidence'}])}<p class="sub">${escapeHtml(x.caveat)}</p>`:`<p>${escapeHtml(x.reason)}</p>`};}
}

function renderTrends(){const tasks=taskRows(state.model,state.projId);if(!state.selectedTrendTask)state.selectedTrendTask=tasks[0]?.task_code||tasks[0]?.task_id||'';const stability=forecastStability(state.revisionRepository,state.selectedTrendTask),timeline=activityTimeline(state.revisionRepository,state.selectedTrendTask),confidence=forecastConfidence(state.revisionRepository,state.selectedTrendTask),stalled=stalledActivities(state.revisionRepository,{thresholdPercent:Number(state.settings.stalledThresholdPercent||90),minPeriods:Number(state.settings.stalledPeriods||3)});const ${escapeHtml(t.task_name||'')}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="cards">${card('Stability Score',`${stability.score}/100`)}${card('Confidence',confidence.confidence)}${card('Avg function modelFromPackage(pkg){const tables=new Map((pkg.tables||[]).map(([n,t])=>[n,{name:t.name||n,fields:[...(t.fields||[])],rows:(t.rows||[]).map(r=>({...r}))}]));const m=new XERModel({header:pkg.header||[],tables,warnings:[],sourceText:''});m.sourceFormat=pkg.sourceFormat||'xer';return m}

export class AuditLog{constructor(){this.items=[]}add(action,details={}){const e={id:`a-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,5)}`,at:new Date().toISOString(),action,details};this.items.unshift(e);if(this.items.length>500)this.items.length=500;return e}export(){return JSON.stringify({schema:'uss-audit-v1',items:this.items},null,2)}clear(){this.items=[]}}


===== FILE: src/baselines.js =====

import { taskRows, taskStart, taskFinish } from './semantic.js';
import { num } from './parser.js';
const DAY=86400000;
function key(t){return String(t.task_code||t.task_id||'')}
export function makeBaseline(model,projId,{id,name,fileName,role='Unassigned',importedAt=new Date().toISOString()}={}){
  const project=model.find('PROJECT','proj_id',projId)||model.table('PROJECT')[0]||{};
  return {id:id||`bl-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`,name:name||project.proj_short_name||project.proj_name||fileName||'Baseline',fileName:fileName||'',role,importedAt,model,projId:projId||project.proj_id||null};
}
export function baselineTaskMap(b){return new Map(taskRows(b.model,b.projId).map(t=>[key(t),t]))}
export function matchBaselineTask(task,b){return baselineTaskMap(b).get(key(task))||null}
function days(a,b){if(!a||!b)return null;return (a-b)/DAY}
export function baselineRows(currentModel,currentProjId,b,hoursPerDay=8){
  const cur=taskRows(currentModel,currentProjId), base=taskRows(b.model,b.projId), bm=new Map(base.map(t=>[key(t),t])), cm=new Map(cur.map(t=>[key(t),t]));
  const make=(t,bt,status)=>{const cs=t?taskStart(t):null,cf=t?taskFinish(t):null,bs=bt?taskStart(bt):null,bf=bt?taskFinish(bt):null;return {task_id:t?.task_id||'',activity:t?.task_code||bt?.task_code||bt?.task_id||'',name:t?.task_name||bt?.task_name||'',status,matched:!!t&&!!bt,current_start:cs,current_finish:cf,baseline_start:bs,baseline_finish:bf,start_variance_days:t&&bt?days(cs,bs):null,finish_variance_days:t&&bt?days(cf,bf):null,current_duration_days:t?num(t.target_drtn_hr_cnt)/(hoursPerDay||8):null,baseline_duration_days:bt?num(bt.target_drtn_hr_cnt)/(hoursPerDay||8):null,duration_variance_days:t&&bt?(num(t.target_drtn_hr_cnt)-num(bt.target_drtn_hr_cnt))/(hoursPerDay||8):null,current_float_days:t?num(t.total_float_hr_cnt)/(hoursPerDay||8):null,baseline_float_days:bt?num(bt.total_float_hr_cnt)/(hoursPerDay||8):null,float_variance_days:t&&bt?(num(t.total_float_hr_cnt)-num(bt.total_float_hr_cnt))/(hoursPerDay||8):null}};
  const rows=cur.map(t=>{const bt=bm.get(key(t));return make(t,bt,bt?'Matched':'Current only')});
  for(const bt of base)if(!cm.has(key(bt)))rows.push(make(null,bt,'Baseline only'));
  return rows;
}
export function baselineSummary(currentModel,currentProjId,b,hoursPerDay=8){const rows=baselineRows(currentModel,currentProjId,b,hoursPerDay);const matched=rows.filter(r=>r.matched);const f=matched.map(r=>r.finish_variance_days).filter(Number.isFinite);return {current:rows.length,baseline:taskRows(b.model,b.projId).length,matched:matched.length,unmatchedCurrent:rows.filter(r=>r.status==='Current only').length,unmatchedBaseline:rows.filter(r=>r.status==='Baseline only').length,late:matched.filter(r=>(r.finish_variance_days??0)>0.001).length,ahead:matched.filter(r=>(r.finish_variance_days??0)<-0.001).length,avgFinishVarianceDays:f.length?f.reduce((a,x)=>a+x,0)/f.length:0}}
export function baselineOverlayMap(b){const m=new Map();for(const t of taskRows(b.model,b.projId))m.set(key(t),{start:taskStart(t),finish:taskFinish(t),task:t});return m}
export function setRole(baselines,id,role){for(const b of baselines){if(b.id===id)b.role=role;else if(role!=='Unassigned'&&b.role===role)b.role='Unassigned'}return baselines}


===== FILE: src/bim-advanced.js =====

import { allActivityBimLinks } from './bim-links.js';
export function reconcileModelLinks(model,oldMeta,newMeta){const oldIds=new Set((oldMeta?.objects||[]).map(o=>o.objectId||o.guid||o.externalId||o.id)),newIds=new Set((newMeta?.objects||[]).map(o=>o.objectId||o.guid||o.externalId||o.id));const projId=model.table('PROJECT')[0]?.proj_id||model.table('TASK')[0]?.proj_id||'';const groups=allActivityBimLinks(model,projId);const refs=groups.flatMap(g=>g.links.map(l=>({...l,taskId:g.taskId,taskCode:g.taskCode})));let exact=0,missing=0;const missingRefs=[];for(const x of refs){const id=x.objectId||x.guid||x.externalId;if(newIds.has(id))exact++;else{missing++;missingRefs.push(x)}}return {checked:refs.length,exact,missing,missingRefs,oldObjects:oldIds.size,newObjects:newIds.size}}
export function buildBimSets(meta,property,values){return values.map(v=>({id:`set-${property}-${v}`,name:`${property}: ${v}`,property,value:v,objectIds:(meta?.objects||[]).filter(o=>String(o[property]??'')===String(v)).map(o=>o.objectId||o.guid||o.externalId||o.id)}))}
export function matchBimRule(meta,rule){return (meta?.objects||[]).filter(o=>(rule.conditions||[]).every(c=>String(o[c.property]??'').toLowerCase()===String(c.value??'').toLowerCase()))}
export function spatialCongestion({activities=[],linksByTask=new Map(),threshold=3}){const buckets=new Map();for(const a of activities){const day=String(a.early_start_date||a.target_start_date||'').slice(0,10);for(const l of linksByTask.get(String(a.task_id))||[]){const zone=l.level||l.zone||'Unzoned',k=`${day}|${zone}`;buckets.set(k,(buckets.get(k)||0)+1)}}return [...buckets.entries()].filter(([,n])=>n>=threshold).map(([k,count])=>{const [date,zone]=k.split('|');return {date,zone,count}})}


===== FILE: src/bim-links.js =====

/**
 * BIM ↔ P6 activity linking.
 *
 * Design:
 * - Canonical lightweight activity links are persisted inside the XER using
 *   Activity text UDFs (UDFTYPE/UDFVALUE), so links travel with the schedule.
 * - Rich BIM metadata stays in an optional local .bimlink.json sidecar.
 * - Links use stable model/object identifiers, never transient viewer dbIds.
 */

export const BIM_UDF_PREFIX = 'BIM_LINK_';
export const BIM_UDF_DATA_TYPE = 'FT_TEXT';
export const BIM_UDF_CHUNK = 240; // comfortably below P6 text-UDF limits
export const BIM_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

function touch(model){ model.indexes = new Map(); }
function ensureTable(model,name,fields){
  if(!model.tables.has(name)) model.tables.set(name,{name,fields:[...fields],rows:[]});
  const t=model.tables.get(name); for(const f of fields) if(!t.fields.includes(f)) t.fields.push(f); return t;
}
function nextNumericId(rows,field,start=1){let max=start-1;for(const r of rows){const n=Number(r[field]);if(Number.isFinite(n))max=Math.max(max,n)}return String(max+1)}
function fieldLabel(i){return `${BIM_UDF_PREFIX}${String(i).padStart(2,'0')}`}
function encodePart(v){return encodeURIComponent(String(v??'').trim()).replace(/%7E/gi,'~')}
function decodePart(v){try{return decodeURIComponent(v)}catch{return v}}

export function normalizeBimLink(link){
  const modelKey=String(link?.modelKey??link?.model_id??link?.model??'').trim();
  const objectId=String(link?.objectId??link?.externalId??link?.external_id??link?.guid??link?.id??'').trim();
  if(!modelKey||!objectId) return null;
  return {modelKey,objectId};
}
export function dedupeBimLinks(links=[]){
  const out=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const raw of links){const x=normalizeBimLink(raw);if(!x)continue;const k=`${x.modelKey}\u0000${x.objectId}`;if(seen.has(k))continue;seen.add(k);out.push(x)}
  return out;
}

export function encodeBimLinks(links=[]){
  const clean=dedupeBimLinks(links);
  return clean.length?`v1|${clean.map(x=>`${encodePart(x.modelKey)}~${encodePart(x.objectId)}`).join(';')}`:'v1|';
}
export function decodeBimLinks(payload=''){
  const text=String(payload||'').trim(); if(!text)return [];
  const body=text.startsWith('v1|')?text.slice(3):text;
  return dedupeBimLinks(body.split(';').filter(Boolean).map(token=>{const i=token.indexOf('~');return i<0?null:{modelKey:decodePart(token.slice(0,i)),objectId:decodePart(token.slice(i+1))}}));
}

/** Split at whole-link boundaries where possible; each UDFVALUE stays compact. */
export function chunkBimPayload(links=[],maxLen=BIM_UDF_CHUNK){
  const clean=dedupeBimLinks(links); if(!clean.length)return [];
  const tokens=clean.map(x=>`${encodePart(x.modelKey)}~${encodePart(x.objectId)}`);
  const chunks=[];let cur='v1|';
  for(const token of tokens){
    const addition=(cur==='v1|'?'':';')+token;
    if(cur.length+addition.length<=maxLen){cur+=addition;continue}
    if(cur!=='v1|')chunks.push(cur);
    // Very long stable IDs are still preserved; serializers do not truncate.
    cur='v1|'+token;
  }
  if(cur!=='v1|')chunks.push(cur); return chunks;
}

function taskBimUdfTypes(model){
  return (model.table('UDFTYPE')||[]).filter(u=>String(u.table_name||'').toUpperCase()==='TASK'&&String(u.udf_type_label||'').startsWith(BIM_UDF_PREFIX));
}
export function ensureBimUdfTypes(model,count){
  const t=ensureTable(model,'UDFTYPE',['udf_type_id','table_name','udf_type_name','udf_type_label','logical_data_type','super_flag','indicator_expression','summary_indicator_expression','export_flag']);
  const existing=new Map(taskBimUdfTypes(model).map(u=>[u.udf_type_label,u]));
  const out=[];
  for(let i=1;i<=count;i++){
    const label=fieldLabel(i);let row=existing.get(label);
    if(!row){const id=nextNumericId(t.rows,'udf_type_id',1);row=Object.fromEntries(t.fields.map(f=>[f,'']));Object.assign(row,{udf_type_id:id,table_name:'TASK',udf_type_name:`user_field_${id}`,udf_type_label:label,logical_data_type:BIM_UDF_DATA_TYPE,super_flag:'N',export_flag:'Y'});t.rows.push(row)}
    else {row.table_name='TASK';row.logical_data_type=BIM_UDF_DATA_TYPE;row.super_flag=row.super_flag||'N';if('export_flag'in row)row.export_flag=row.export_flag||'Y'}
    out.push(row);
  }
  touch(model);return out;
}

export function getActivityBimLinks(model,taskId){
  const types=taskBimUdfTypes(model).sort((a,b)=>String(a.udf_type_label).localeCompare(String(b.udf_type_label),undefined,{numeric:true}));
  if(!types.length)return [];
  const ids=new Set(types.map(x=>String(x.udf_type_id))), order=new Map(types.map((x,i)=>[String(x.udf_type_id),i]));
  const vals=(model.table('UDFVALUE')||[]).filter(v=>String(v.fk_id)===String(taskId)&&ids.has(String(v.udf_type_id))).sort((a,b)=>(order.get(String(a.udf_type_id))??0)-(order.get(String(b.udf_type_id))??0));
  const merged=[];for(const v of vals){const links=decodeBimLinks(v.udf_text||'');for(const x of links)merged.push(x)}return dedupeBimLinks(merged);
}

export function setActivityBimLinks(model,projId,taskId,links=[]){
  const task=model.find('TASK','task_id',String(taskId));if(!task)throw new Error('Activity not found.');
  if(String(task.proj_id)!==String(projId))throw new Error('Activity does not belong to the selected project.');
  const chunks=chunkBimPayload(links);
  const types=ensureBimUdfTypes(model,Math.max(1,chunks.length));
  const allTypeIds=new Set(taskBimUdfTypes(model).map(x=>String(x.udf_type_id)));
  const t=ensureTable(model,'UDFVALUE',['udf_type_id','fk_id','proj_id','udf_code_id','udf_date','udf_number','udf_text']);
  t.rows=t.rows.filter(v=>!(String(v.fk_id)===String(taskId)&&allTypeIds.has(String(v.udf_type_id))));
  chunks.forEach((payload,i)=>{const u=types[i];const row=Object.fromEntries(t.fields.map(f=>[f,'']));Object.assign(row,{udf_type_id:String(u.udf_type_id),fk_id:String(taskId),proj_id:String(projId),udf_text:payload});t.rows.push(row)});
  touch(model);return dedupeBimLinks(links);
}
export function addActivityBimLinks(model,projId,taskId,links=[]){return setActivityBimLinks(model,projId,taskId,[...getActivityBimLinks(model,taskId),...links])}
export function removeActivityBimLinks(model,projId,taskId,links=[]){const remove=new Set(dedupeBimLinks(links).map(x=>`${x.modelKey}\u0000${x.objectId}`));return setActivityBimLinks(model,projId,taskId,getActivityBimLinks(model,taskId).filter(x=>!remove.has(`${x.modelKey}\u0000${x.objectId}`)))}

export function allActivityBimLinks(model,projId){
  return (model.table('TASK')||[]).filter(t=>String(t.proj_id)===String(projId)).map(t=>({taskId:String(t.task_id),taskCode:t.task_code||'',taskName:t.task_name||'',links:getActivityBimLinks(model,t.task_id)})).filter(x=>x.links.length);
}

export function makeBimSidecar(model,projId,{models=[]}={}){
  const p=model.find('PROJECT','proj_id',String(projId));
  return {schema:'p6-xer-bim-links',schemaVersion:BIM_SCHEMA_VERSION,project:{projId:String(projId),projectCode:p?.proj_short_name||'',projectName:p?.proj_name||''},models,activityLinks:allActivityBimLinks(model,projId),exportedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
}
export function applyBimSidecar(model,projId,sidecar,{replace=true}={}){
  if(!sidecar||sidecar.schema!=='p6-xer-bim-links')throw new Error('Not a P6 BIM link sidecar file.');
  let count=0;
  for(const a of sidecar.activityLinks||[]){const task=model.find('TASK','task_id',String(a.taskId));if(!task||String(task.proj_id)!==String(projId))continue;const links=replace?dedupeBimLinks(a.links):dedupeBimLinks([...getActivityBimLinks(model,a.taskId),...(a.links||[])]);setActivityBimLinks(model,projId,a.taskId,links);count+=links.length}
  return {activityCount:(sidecar.activityLinks||[]).length,linkCount:count,models:Array.isArray(sidecar.models)?sidecar.models:[]};
}

/** Normalize a simple local web-model metadata bundle for the link manager. */
export function normalizeModelMetadata(data,fileName='model.json'){
  const source=data?.model||data||{};
  const modelKey=String(source.modelKey||source.model_id||source.id||source.guid||fileName.replace(/\.json$/i,'')).trim();
  const name=String(source.name||source.modelName||source.title||modelKey);
  const version=String(source.version||source.revision||source.modelVersion||'');
  const rawObjects=Array.isArray(source.objects)?source.objects:Array.isArray(data?.objects)?data.objects:[];
  const objects=rawObjects.map((o,i)=>{
    const objectId=String(o.externalId||o.external_id||o.sourceGuid||o.guid||o.uniqueId||o.id||'').trim();
    if(!objectId)return null;
    return {objectId,name:String(o.name||o.displayName||o.label||`Object ${i+1}`),category:String(o.category||o.type||''),level:String(o.level||o.storey||o.floor||''),system:String(o.system||o.systemName||''),sourceFile:String(o.sourceFile||o.file||''),path:String(o.path||o.hierarchyPath||''),properties:o.properties&&typeof o.properties==='object'?o.properties:{}};
  }).filter(Boolean);
  return {modelKey,name,version,sourceFile:String(source.sourceFile||fileName),objects};
}


===== FILE: src/bim-viewer.js =====

/* Local GLB viewer for the GitHub Pages app.
